backend/src/providers/songapi.cpp （78 行）
#include "museflow/providers.hpp"
#include "museflow/runtime.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
#include <fstream>

namespace museflow {
namespace {
bool safeId(const std::string& id) {
    return !id.empty() && id.size() <= 256 && std::all_of(id.begin(), id.end(), [](unsigned char c) {
        return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '_' || c == '-';
    });
}
// Count UTF-16 units, matching the API's string limits; never truncate user lyrics silently.
size_t characters(const std::string& text) {
    size_t count = 0;
    for (unsigned char c : text) if ((c & 0xc0) != 0x80) count += c >= 0xf0 ? 2 : 1;
    return count;
}
}
SongApiProvider::SongApiProvider(MusicSettings settings, std::filesystem::path directory, const std::atomic_bool* cancelled)
    : settings_(std::move(settings)), directory_(std::move(directory)), cancelled_(cancelled) {}
TestResult SongApiProvider::testConnection() {
    auto response = requestSongApiJson("/v1/generations?limit=1", settings_.apiKey, nullptr, {10, 30, cancelled_});
    if (!response.is_object() || response.value("object", "") != "list" || !response.contains("data") || !response["data"].is_array())
        throw ProviderError("invalid_response", "SongAPI 未返回有效记录列表，请检查服务状态");
    return {true, settings_.apiKey.starts_with("sk_test_")
        ? "SongAPI 测试 Key 验证成功；生成只返回静音示例，不扣积分。此检查未生成音乐。"
        : "SongAPI 连接与 Key 验证成功；此检查未生成音乐、不扣生成积分，余额和模型权限以实际生成为准。"};
}
GenerationTask SongApiProvider::generate(const MusicGenerateRequest& request) {
    const auto& b = request.blueprint;
    if (characters(b.finalLyriaPrompt) > 3000) throw ProviderError("prompt_too_long", "SongAPI 音乐提示词最多 3000 字符，请缩短场景或结构描述", 400);
    if (characters(b.negativePrompt) > 500) throw ProviderError("prompt_too_long", "SongAPI 避免元素最多 500 字符，请缩短内容", 400);
    if (b.vocalMode != "instrumental" && characters(b.lyrics) > 5000) throw ProviderError("lyrics_too_long", "SongAPI 歌词最多 5000 字符，请缩短歌词", 400);
    json body{{"prompt", b.finalLyriaPrompt}, {"title", b.title}, {"model", settings_.model},
        {"instrumental", b.vocalMode == "instrumental"}, {"negative_tags", b.negativePrompt}};
    if (b.vocalMode != "instrumental" && !b.lyrics.empty()) body["lyrics"] = b.lyrics;
    auto response = requestSongApiJson("/v1/generate", settings_.apiKey, &body, {10, 60, cancelled_});
    try {
        id_ = response.at("id").get<std::string>();
        if (!safeId(id_)) throw ProviderError("invalid_response", "SongAPI 未返回有效任务 ID；请在控制台确认任务后再决定是否重试");
        // Always poll the accepted id, even when submission already reports completion.
        return {id_};
    } catch (const json::exception&) { throw ProviderError("invalid_response", "SongAPI 返回的任务结构无效；请在控制台确认任务后再决定是否重试"); }
}
GenerationStatus SongApiProvider::queryTask(const std::string& id) {
    if (id != id_) throw ProviderError("invalid_task", "SongAPI 任务 ID 不匹配");
    if (status_.state == "completed") return status_;
    const auto response = requestSongApiJson("/v1/generations/" + id, settings_.apiKey, nullptr, {10, 60, cancelled_});
    try {
        if (response.at("id") != id) throw ProviderError("invalid_response", "SongAPI 返回的任务 ID 不匹配");
        const auto state = response.at("status").get<std::string>();
        if (state == "queued" || state == "processing") return {"generating", state == "queued" ? 25 : 50, "", 0};
        if (state == "failed") throw ProviderError("generation_failed", "SongAPI 音乐任务失败，请在控制台查看原因及积分退还情况；未自动重新生成");
        if (state != "complete") throw ProviderError("invalid_response", "SongAPI 返回了未知任务状态");
        std::string address;
        if (response.contains("audio_url") && response["audio_url"].is_string()) address = response["audio_url"];
        const json* clip = nullptr;
        if (response.contains("clips") && response["clips"].is_array() && !response["clips"].empty()) {
            clip = &response["clips"][0];
            if (address.empty() && clip->contains("audio_url") && (*clip)["audio_url"].is_string()) address = (*clip)["audio_url"];
        }
        if (address.empty()) throw ProviderError("missing_audio", "SongAPI 任务已完成，但未返回音频地址");
        const auto bytes = downloadSongApiAudio(address, {10, 120, cancelled_});
        if (bytes.empty()) throw ProviderError("invalid_audio", "SongAPI 返回了空音频");
        GenerationStatus result{"completed", 80, "", 0};
        result.format = bytes.starts_with("RIFF") ? "wav" : "mp3";
        if (clip && clip->contains("lyrics") && (*clip)["lyrics"].is_string()) result.songText.push_back((*clip)["lyrics"]);
        std::filesystem::create_directories(directory_);
        const auto path = directory_ / (uuid() + "." + result.format);
        std::ofstream output(path, std::ios::binary); output.write(bytes.data(), bytes.size()); output.close();
        if (!output) { std::error_code error; std::filesystem::remove(path, error); throw ProviderError("audio_write", "无法保存 SongAPI 音频，请检查磁盘空间及目录权限"); }
        result.audioPath = path.string();
        status_ = result;
        return status_;
    } catch (const json::exception&) { throw ProviderError("invalid_response", "SongAPI 任务结果格式无效"); }
}
}
backend/src/repositories/database.cpp （196 行）
#include "museflow/database.hpp"
#include "museflow/runtime.hpp"

namespace museflow {
namespace {
void execute(sqlite3* db, const char* sql) {
    if (sqlite3_exec(db, sql, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr) != SQLITE_OK) throw std::runtime_error("数据库操作失败");
}
class Statement {
    sqlite3_stmt* value_ = nullptr;
public:
    Statement(sqlite3* db, const char* sql) {
        if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, sql, -1, &value_, nullptr) != SQLITE_OK) throw std::runtime_error("数据库查询准备失败");
    }
    ~Statement() { sqlite3_finalize(value_); }
    void bind(int index, const std::string& value) {
        if (sqlite3_bind_text(value_, index, value.c_str(), static_cast<int>(value.size()), SQLITE_TRANSIENT) != SQLITE_OK) throw std::runtime_error("数据库参数绑定失败");
    }
    bool next() {
        int result = sqlite3_step(value_);
        if (result != SQLITE_ROW && result != SQLITE_DONE) throw std::runtime_error("数据库读写失败");
        return result == SQLITE_ROW;
    }
    std::string text(int index) {
        const auto* result = sqlite3_column_text(value_, index);
        return result ? reinterpret_cast<const char*>(result) : "";
    }
};
json record(sqlite3* db, const char* sql, const std::string& id) {
    Statement query(db, sql); query.bind(1, id);
    if (!query.next()) throw NotFound("记录不存在");
    return json::parse(query.text(0));
}
void writeTask(sqlite3* db, const json& task) {
    Statement query(db, "UPDATE tasks SET status=?, payload=? WHERE id=?");
    query.bind(1, task.at("status")); query.bind(2, task.dump()); query.bind(3, task.at("id")); query.next();
}
}
Database::Database(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
    if (sqlite3_open(path.c_str(), &db_) != SQLITE_OK) {
        sqlite3_close(db_); db_ = nullptr; throw std::runtime_error("无法打开数据库");
    }
    try {
        sqlite3_busy_timeout(db_, 5000);
        execute(db_, "PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON; PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; PRAGMA user_version=1;"
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, payload TEXT NOT NULL);"
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, project_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, status TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL);"
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS versions (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, project_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, task_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES tasks(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, payload TEXT NOT NULL);"
            "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS tasks_project ON tasks(project_id);"
            "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS versions_project ON versions(project_id);");
        Statement pending(db_, "SELECT payload FROM tasks WHERE status NOT IN ('completed','failed')");
        std::vector<json> interrupted;
        while (pending.next()) interrupted.push_back(json::parse(pending.text(0)));
        for (auto& task : interrupted) {
            task["status"] = "failed"; task["error"] = "程序已重启，上次生成中断，请重新生成"; task["updatedAt"] = timestamp(); writeTask(db_, task);
        }
    } catch (...) { sqlite3_close(db_); db_ = nullptr; throw; }
}
Database::~Database() { sqlite3_close(db_); }
json Database::projects() {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    Statement query(db_, "SELECT payload FROM projects ORDER BY json_extract(payload,'$.updatedAt') DESC, rowid DESC");
    json result = json::array(); while (query.next()) result.push_back(json::parse(query.text(0))); return result;
}
json Database::project(const std::string& id) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    auto result = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM projects WHERE id=?", id);
    Statement query(db_, "SELECT payload FROM tasks WHERE project_id=? ORDER BY rowid DESC"); query.bind(1, id);
    result["tasks"] = json::array(); while (query.next()) result["tasks"].push_back(json::parse(query.text(0))); return result;
}
json Database::saveProject(const json& payload, const std::string& id) {
    auto blueprint = parseBlueprint(payload.at("blueprint"));
    auto name = payload.value("name", blueprint.title);
    if (name.empty() || name.size() > 300) throw ValidationError("项目名称不能为空，且不能超过 300 字节");
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    json result;
    if (id.empty()) result = {{"id", uuid()}, {"createdAt", timestamp()}};
    else result = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM projects WHERE id=?", id);
    // Whitelist persisted fields: never persist arbitrary request properties or secrets.
    result["name"] = name; result["blueprint"] = blueprint; result["sceneDescription"] = blueprint.sceneDescription; result["updatedAt"] = timestamp();
    if (payload.contains("draftParentVersionId")) result["draftParentVersionId"] = payload["draftParentVersionId"];
    if (result.contains("draftParentVersionId") && !result["draftParentVersionId"].is_null()) {
        const auto parentId = result["draftParentVersionId"].get<std::string>();
        if (!isUuid(parentId)) throw ValidationError("父版本 ID 无效");
        auto parent = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM versions WHERE id=?", parentId);
        if (parent["projectId"] != result["id"]) throw ValidationError("父版本必须属于同一项目");
    }
    Statement query(db_, "INSERT INTO projects(id,payload) VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET payload=excluded.payload");
    query.bind(1, result["id"]); query.bind(2, result.dump()); query.next(); return result;
}
void Database::deleteProject(const std::string& id) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM projects WHERE id=?", id);
    Statement active(db_, "SELECT id FROM tasks WHERE project_id=? AND status NOT IN ('completed','failed')"); active.bind(1, id);
    if (active.next()) throw ValidationError("项目仍有生成任务，请等待完成后删除");
    Statement query(db_, "DELETE FROM projects WHERE id=?"); query.bind(1, id); query.next();
}
void Database::createTask(const json& task) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM projects WHERE id=?", task.at("projectId"));
    if (task.contains("parentVersionId") && !task["parentVersionId"].is_null()) {
        auto parent = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM versions WHERE id=?", task["parentVersionId"]);
        if (parent["projectId"] != task["projectId"]) throw ValidationError("不能从其他项目的版本创建分支");
    }
    Statement query(db_, "INSERT INTO tasks(id,project_id,status,payload) VALUES(?,?,?,?)");
    query.bind(1, task.at("id")); query.bind(2, task.at("projectId")); query.bind(3, task.at("status")); query.bind(4, task.dump()); query.next();
}
json Database::task(const std::string& id) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_); return record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM tasks WHERE id=?", id);
}
void Database::updateTask(const std::string& id, const std::string& status, int progress, const std::string& error) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    auto task = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM tasks WHERE id=?", id);
    task["status"] = status; task["progress"] = progress; task["error"] = error; task["updatedAt"] = timestamp(); writeTask(db_, task);
}
void Database::completeTask(const std::string& id, const json& version) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    auto task = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM tasks WHERE id=?", id);
    task["status"] = "completed"; task["progress"] = 100; task["versionId"] = version.at("id"); task["actualDurationSeconds"] = version.at("actualDurationSeconds"); task["updatedAt"] = timestamp();
    for (const auto* field : {"format", "sourceDurationSeconds", "strictDurationApplied", "songText", "songStructure"})
        if (version.contains(field)) task[field] = version[field];
    execute(db_, "BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
    try {
        Statement query(db_, "INSERT INTO versions(id,project_id,task_id,payload) VALUES(?,?,?,?)");
        query.bind(1, version.at("id")); query.bind(2, task.at("projectId")); query.bind(3, id); query.bind(4, version.dump()); query.next();
        writeTask(db_, task); execute(db_, "COMMIT");
    } catch (...) { execute(db_, "ROLLBACK"); throw; }
}
json Database::versions(const std::string& projectId) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    Statement query(db_, "SELECT payload FROM versions WHERE (?='' OR project_id=?) ORDER BY rowid DESC");
    query.bind(1, projectId); query.bind(2, projectId);
    json result = json::array(); while (query.next()) result.push_back(json::parse(query.text(0))); return result;
}

json Database::version(const std::string& id) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_); return record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM versions WHERE id=?", id);
}
int Database::activeTaskCount() {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    Statement query(db_, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tasks WHERE status NOT IN ('completed','failed')"); query.next(); return std::stoi(query.text(0));
}
json Database::branchVersion(const std::string& id) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    auto parent = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM versions WHERE id=?", id);
    const std::string projectId = parent["projectId"];
    auto project = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM projects WHERE id=?", projectId);
    Statement active(db_, "SELECT id FROM tasks WHERE project_id=? AND status NOT IN ('completed','failed')"); active.bind(1, projectId);
    if (active.next()) throw ValidationError("该项目仍有生成任务，请完成后再创建分支草稿");
    project["blueprint"] = parent["blueprint"]; project["blueprint"]["id"] = uuid();
    project["sceneDescription"] = parent["blueprint"]["sceneDescription"];
    project["draftParentVersionId"] = id; project["updatedAt"] = timestamp();
    Statement update(db_, "UPDATE projects SET payload=? WHERE id=?"); update.bind(1, project.dump()); update.bind(2, projectId); update.next();
    return project;
}
json Database::updateVersionMetadata(const std::string& id, const json& patch) {
    if (!patch.is_object()) throw ValidationError("版本评价必须是对象");
    for (auto it = patch.begin(); it != patch.end(); ++it)
        if (it.key() != "rating" && it.key() != "notes") throw ValidationError("只能修改评分和备注，版本参数不可覆盖");
    if (patch.contains("rating") && (!patch["rating"].is_number_integer() || patch["rating"].get<double>() < 0 || patch["rating"].get<double>() > 5)) throw ValidationError("评分必须是 0～5 的整数，0 表示未评分");
    if (patch.contains("notes") && (!patch["notes"].is_string() || patch["notes"].get_ref<const std::string&>().size() > 6000)) throw ValidationError("备注不能超过 6000 字节");
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_); auto version = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM versions WHERE id=?", id);
    version.update(patch); version["metadataUpdatedAt"] = timestamp();
    Statement query(db_, "UPDATE versions SET payload=? WHERE id=?"); query.bind(1, version.dump()); query.bind(2, id); query.next(); return version;
}
json Database::deleteVersion(const std::string& id) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_); auto version = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM versions WHERE id=?", id);
    Statement children(db_, "SELECT id FROM versions WHERE json_extract(payload,'$.parentVersionId')=?"); children.bind(1, id);
    if (children.next()) throw ValidationError("该版本仍有分支作品，请先删除末端分支，或删除整个项目");
    Statement active(db_, "SELECT id FROM tasks WHERE json_extract(payload,'$.parentVersionId')=? AND status NOT IN ('completed','failed')"); active.bind(1, id);
    if (active.next()) throw ValidationError("该版本仍有正在生成的分支，请等待完成后再删除");
    const std::string taskId = version["generationId"], projectId = version["projectId"];
    auto task = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM tasks WHERE id=?", taskId);
    auto project = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM projects WHERE id=?", projectId);
    task["audioDeleted"] = true; task["updatedAt"] = timestamp();
    if (project.value("draftParentVersionId", json(nullptr)) == id) project["draftParentVersionId"] = nullptr;
    execute(db_, "BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
    try {
        Statement remove(db_, "DELETE FROM versions WHERE id=?"); remove.bind(1, id); remove.next();
        writeTask(db_, task);
        Statement update(db_, "UPDATE projects SET payload=? WHERE id=?"); update.bind(1, project.dump()); update.bind(2, projectId); update.next();
        execute(db_, "COMMIT");
    } catch (...) { execute(db_, "ROLLBACK"); throw; }
    return version;
}
json Database::exportProject(const std::string& id) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    auto project = record(db_, "SELECT payload FROM projects WHERE id=?", id);
    json tasks = json::array(), versions = json::array();
    Statement taskQuery(db_, "SELECT payload FROM tasks WHERE project_id=? ORDER BY rowid"); taskQuery.bind(1, id);
    while (taskQuery.next()) tasks.push_back(json::parse(taskQuery.text(0)));
    Statement versionQuery(db_, "SELECT payload FROM versions WHERE project_id=? ORDER BY rowid"); versionQuery.bind(1, id);
    while (versionQuery.next()) versions.push_back(json::parse(versionQuery.text(0)));
    return {{"format", "museflow-project"}, {"schemaVersion", 1}, {"exportedAt", timestamp()}, {"project", project}, {"tasks", tasks}, {"versions", versions}, {"includesAudio", false}};
}
}
backend/src/services/audio_processing.cpp （83 行）
#include "museflow/audio_processing.hpp"
#include <spawn.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <csignal>
#include <chrono>
#include <thread>
#include <cmath>
#include <cerrno>
extern char** environ;
namespace museflow {
namespace {
// No shell, bounded stdout, child receives no API keys, cancellation/timeout always reaps the child.
std::string run(std::vector<std::string> args, int timeout, const std::atomic_bool* cancelled) {
    int pipes[2];
    if (pipe2(pipes, O_CLOEXEC | O_NONBLOCK)) throw ProviderError("audio_process", "无法创建音频处理管道");
    posix_spawn_file_actions_t actions; posix_spawn_file_actions_init(&actions);
    posix_spawn_file_actions_adddup2(&actions, pipes[1], STDOUT_FILENO);
    posix_spawn_file_actions_addopen(&actions, STDERR_FILENO, "/dev/null", O_WRONLY, 0);
    posix_spawn_file_actions_addopen(&actions, STDIN_FILENO, "/dev/null", O_RDONLY, 0);
    posix_spawnattr_t attributes; posix_spawnattr_init(&attributes);
    sigset_t empty; sigemptyset(&empty); posix_spawnattr_setsigmask(&attributes, &empty);
    posix_spawnattr_setflags(&attributes, POSIX_SPAWN_SETSIGMASK);
    std::vector<char*> argv; for (auto& arg : args) argv.push_back(arg.data()); argv.push_back(nullptr);
    std::vector<char*> environment;
    for (char** e = environ; *e; ++e) {
        std::string_view entry(*e);
        if (!entry.starts_with("DEEPSEEK_API_KEY=") && !entry.starts_with("GEMINI_API_KEY=") && !entry.starts_with("SONGAPI_KEY=")) environment.push_back(*e);
    }
    environment.push_back(nullptr);
    pid_t child; int launched = posix_spawnp(&child, argv[0], &actions, &attributes, argv.data(), environment.data());
    posix_spawn_file_actions_destroy(&actions); posix_spawnattr_destroy(&attributes); close(pipes[1]);
    if (launched) { close(pipes[0]); throw ProviderError("missing_ffmpeg", "无法启动 FFmpeg/ffprobe，请安装 ffmpeg 并检查 PATH"); }
    std::string output; const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(timeout);
    int status = 0;
    for (;;) {
        char buffer[4096]; ssize_t count;
        while ((count = read(pipes[0], buffer, sizeof(buffer))) > 0) output.append(buffer, count);
        const auto ended = waitpid(child, &status, WNOHANG);
        if (ended == child) break;
        if ((ended < 0 && errno != EINTR) || output.size() > 65536 || (cancelled && cancelled->load()) || std::chrono::steady_clock::now() > deadline) {
            kill(child, SIGKILL); while (waitpid(child, &status, 0) < 0 && errno == EINTR) {}
            close(pipes[0]); throw ProviderError("audio_timeout", "音频处理已取消或超时，请检查音频文件及系统资源");
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(20));
    }
    close(pipes[0]);
    if (!WIFEXITED(status) || WEXITSTATUS(status)) throw ProviderError("invalid_audio", "FFmpeg 无法解码或处理音频，请检查文件格式及磁盘空间");
    return output;
}
double probe(const std::filesystem::path& file, const std::string& format, const std::atomic_bool* cancelled) {
    auto result = run({"ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-protocol_whitelist", "file,pipe", "-f", format, "-i", file.string(),
        "-show_entries", "format=duration", "-of", "json"}, 30, cancelled);
    try {
        auto value = json::parse(result); auto duration = std::stod(value.at("format").at("duration").get<std::string>());
        if (!std::isfinite(duration) || duration <= 0 || duration > 1200) throw std::runtime_error("range");
        return duration;
    } catch (...) { throw ProviderError("invalid_duration", "音频时长无效或超过 20 分钟处理上限"); }
}
}
ProcessedAudio processAudio(const std::filesystem::path& source, const std::filesystem::path& destination,
    const std::string& sourceFormat, const std::string& outputFormat, bool strict, double target, const std::atomic_bool* cancelled) {
    if ((sourceFormat != "wav" && sourceFormat != "mp3") || (outputFormat != "wav" && outputFormat != "mp3")) throw ValidationError("音频格式无效");
    if (strict && (outputFormat != "wav" || !std::isfinite(target) || target < 5 || target > 600)) throw ValidationError("严格时长使用 WAV，目标范围为 5～600 秒");
    const auto original = probe(source, sourceFormat, cancelled);
    std::vector<std::string> args{"ffmpeg", "-nostdin", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-n", "-protocol_whitelist", "file,pipe", "-f", sourceFormat,
        "-i", source.string(), "-map", "0:a:0", "-vn", "-map_metadata", "-1", "-ar", "44100", "-ac", "2"};
    if (strict) {
        // Fade before padding as well, so a short source never ends abruptly before added silence.
        const double fadeEnd = std::min(original, target), fade = std::min(2.0, fadeEnd / 2);
        args.insert(args.end(), {"-af", "afade=t=out:st=" + std::to_string(fadeEnd - fade) + ":d=" + std::to_string(fade) +
            ",apad,atrim=duration=" + std::to_string(target), "-t", std::to_string(target)});
    }
    if (outputFormat == "wav") args.insert(args.end(), {"-c:a", "pcm_s16le", "-f", "wav"});
    else args.insert(args.end(), {"-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "192k", "-f", "mp3"});
    args.push_back(destination.string());
    run(std::move(args), 120, cancelled);
    auto actual = probe(destination, outputFormat, cancelled);
    if (strict && std::abs(actual - target) > 1.0 / 44100 + 0.000001) throw ProviderError("duration_mismatch", "处理后音频未满足严格时长，作品未保存");
    return {original, actual};
}
}
backend/src/services/generation_queue.cpp （101 行）
#include "museflow/generation_queue.hpp"
#include "museflow/runtime.hpp"
#include "museflow/audio_processing.hpp"

namespace museflow {
GenerationQueue::GenerationQueue(Database& db, std::filesystem::path data, std::unique_ptr<IMusicModelProvider> provider, SettingsService* settings)
    : db_(db), data_(std::move(data)), provider_(std::move(provider)), settings_(settings), worker_([this] { run(); }) {}
GenerationQueue::~GenerationQueue() {
    { std::lock_guard lock(mutex_); stopping_ = true; }
    condition_.notify_all(); worker_.join();
}
json GenerationQueue::enqueue(const std::string& projectId, const MusicBlueprint& blueprint, const std::string& mode, const std::string& parentVersionId) {
    validate(blueprint);
    if (!isUuid(projectId)) throw ValidationError("项目 ID 无效");
    if (mode != "preview" && mode != "full") throw ValidationError("生成模式必须是 preview 或 full");
    if (!parentVersionId.empty() && !isUuid(parentVersionId)) throw ValidationError("父版本 ID 无效");
    auto snapshot = settings_ ? settings_->musicSnapshot() : MusicSettings{};
    if (snapshot.provider == "lyria" && snapshot.apiKey.empty()) throw ProviderError("missing_key", "请先在模型设置输入 Google Gemini API Key，或设置 GEMINI_API_KEY", 400);
    if (snapshot.provider == "songapi" && snapshot.apiKey.empty()) throw ProviderError("missing_key", "请先在模型设置输入 SongAPI Key，或设置 SONGAPI_KEY", 400);
    if (snapshot.provider == "elevenlabs" && snapshot.apiKey.empty()) throw ProviderError("missing_key", "请先在模型设置输入 ElevenLabs API Key，或设置 ELEVENLABS_API_KEY", 400);
    auto rendered = blueprint;
    if (mode == "preview") {
        const double scale = 30.0 / blueprint.targetDurationSeconds;
        rendered.targetDurationSeconds = 30;
        for (auto& section : rendered.structure) { section.startSeconds *= scale; section.endSeconds *= scale; }
        rendered.finalLyriaPrompt = composePrompt(rendered);
    }
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    if (stopping_ || pending_.size() >= 32) throw ValidationError("生成队列已满或正在关闭，请稍后重试");
    json task = {{"id", uuid()}, {"projectId", projectId}, {"blueprint", blueprint}, {"mode", mode}, {"provider", snapshot.provider},
        {"model", snapshot.provider == "mock" ? "MockMusicProvider" : ((snapshot.provider == "songapi" || snapshot.provider == "elevenlabs") ? snapshot.model : (mode == "preview" ? "lyria-3-clip-preview" : "lyria-3.5"))},
        {"generationPrompt", rendered.finalLyriaPrompt}, {"requestedFormat", snapshot.outputFormat}, {"format", blueprint.strictDuration ? "wav" : snapshot.outputFormat},
        {"parentVersionId", parentVersionId.empty() ? json(nullptr) : json(parentVersionId)}, {"status", "queued"}, {"progress", 0}, {"error", ""}, {"createdAt", timestamp()}, {"updatedAt", timestamp()},
        {"modelTargetDurationSeconds", mode == "preview" ? 30.0 : blueprint.targetDurationSeconds}};
    db_.createTask(task); pending_.push_back({task.at("id"), std::move(snapshot)}); condition_.notify_one(); return task;
}
bool GenerationQueue::pause(std::chrono::milliseconds duration) {
    std::unique_lock lock(mutex_); return condition_.wait_for(lock, duration, [this] { return stopping_.load(); });
}
void GenerationQueue::run() {
    for (;;) {
        Job job;
        { std::unique_lock lock(mutex_); condition_.wait(lock, [this] { return stopping_ || !pending_.empty(); });
          if (stopping_) return;
          job = std::move(pending_.front()); pending_.pop_front(); }
        const auto& id = job.id;
        std::filesystem::path destination, original, staging;
        auto fail = [&](const std::string& message) {
            std::error_code error;
            // Preserve a paid model result when local conversion fails; only discard the partial final file.
            if (!destination.empty()) std::filesystem::remove(destination, error);
            try { db_.updateTask(id, "failed", 0, message); } catch (...) {}
        };
        try {
            const auto task = db_.task(id);
            db_.updateTask(id, "preparing", 10);
            if (pause(std::chrono::milliseconds(200))) throw ProviderError("cancelled", "程序关闭，任务已停止；未自动重新生成");
            staging = data_ / "staging" / id;
            std::unique_ptr<IMusicModelProvider> live;
            IMusicModelProvider* provider = provider_.get();
            if (job.settings.provider == "lyria") { live = std::make_unique<LyriaProvider>(job.settings, staging, &stopping_); provider = live.get(); }
            if (job.settings.provider == "songapi") { live = std::make_unique<SongApiProvider>(job.settings, staging, &stopping_); provider = live.get(); }
            if (job.settings.provider == "elevenlabs") { live = std::make_unique<ElevenLabsProvider>(job.settings, staging, &stopping_); provider = live.get(); }
            auto blueprint = task.at("blueprint").get<MusicBlueprint>(); blueprint.finalLyriaPrompt = task.at("generationPrompt");
            db_.updateTask(id, "generating", 20);
            auto remote = provider->generate({blueprint, task.at("mode")});
            GenerationStatus status;
            const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::minutes(15);
            do {
                status = provider->queryTask(remote.id);
                if (status.state == "failed") throw ProviderError("generation_failed", "音乐生成失败，请检查模型设置");
                if (status.state == "completed") break;
                db_.updateTask(id, "generating", status.progress);
                if (pause(std::chrono::milliseconds(live ? 3000 : 180))) throw ProviderError("cancelled", "程序关闭，任务已停止；未自动重新生成");
                if (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() > deadline) throw ProviderError("timeout", "音乐任务超过等待上限；请在服务商账户确认结果后再决定是否重新生成");
            } while (true);
            db_.updateTask(id, "post_processing", 90);
            if (pause(std::chrono::milliseconds(200))) throw ProviderError("cancelled", "程序关闭，音频处理已停止");
            const std::string projectId = task.at("projectId"), format = task.at("format");
            if (!isUuid(projectId) || !isUuid(id)) throw ValidationError("存储标识符无效");
            auto directory = data_ / "projects" / projectId;
            std::filesystem::create_directories(directory);
            destination = directory / (id + "." + format);
            original = directory / (id + ".source." + status.format);
            std::filesystem::copy_file(status.audioPath, original, std::filesystem::copy_options::none);
            auto processed = processAudio(original, destination, status.format, format, blueprint.strictDuration, task.at("modelTargetDurationSeconds"), &stopping_);
            json version = {{"id", uuid()}, {"projectId", projectId}, {"generationId", id}, {"parentVersionId", task.value("parentVersionId", json(nullptr))},
                {"title", task["blueprint"]["title"]}, {"blueprint", task["blueprint"]}, {"mode", task["mode"]},
                {"provider", task["provider"]}, {"model", task["model"]}, {"generationPrompt", task["generationPrompt"]},
                {"format", format}, {"sourceFormat", status.format}, {"requestedFormat", task["requestedFormat"]},
                {"actualDurationSeconds", processed.actualDurationSeconds}, {"sourceDurationSeconds", processed.sourceDurationSeconds},
                {"modelTargetDurationSeconds", task["modelTargetDurationSeconds"]}, {"strictDurationApplied", blueprint.strictDuration},
                {"songText", status.songText}, {"songStructure", status.songStructure},
                {"rating", 0}, {"notes", ""}, {"createdAt", timestamp()}};
            db_.completeTask(id, version);
        } catch (const ProviderError& error) { fail(error.what()); }
        catch (...) { fail("音乐生成或保存失败，请检查音频资源、数据目录权限及磁盘空间"); }
        if (!staging.empty()) { std::error_code error; std::filesystem::remove_all(staging, error); }
    }
}
}
backend/src/services/settings.cpp （157 行）
#include "museflow/settings.hpp"
#include "museflow/runtime.hpp"
#include <fstream>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <set>

namespace museflow {
namespace {
void validKey(const std::string& key) {
    if (key.size() > 8192 || std::any_of(key.begin(), key.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return c < 33 || c > 126; })) throw ValidationError("API Key 格式无效，请勿包含空格或换行");
}
json persisted(const TextSettings& text, const json& music) {
    return {{"version", 1}, {"text", {{"provider", text.provider}, {"baseUrl", text.baseUrl}, {"model", text.model},
        {"connectTimeoutSeconds", text.connectTimeoutSeconds}, {"requestTimeoutSeconds", text.requestTimeoutSeconds}}}, {"music", music}};
}
void applyPreferences(TextSettings& text, json& music, const json& patch) {
    if (!patch.is_object()) throw ValidationError("模型设置必须是 JSON 对象");
    if (patch.contains("text")) {
        const auto& value = patch.at("text");
        if (!value.is_object()) throw ValidationError("创作助手设置必须是对象");
        text.provider = value.value("provider", text.provider); text.baseUrl = value.value("baseUrl", text.baseUrl); text.model = value.value("model", text.model);
        for (const auto* field : {"connectTimeoutSeconds", "requestTimeoutSeconds"})
            if (value.contains(field) && !value[field].is_number_integer()) throw ValidationError("超时秒数必须是整数");
        text.connectTimeoutSeconds = value.value("connectTimeoutSeconds", text.connectTimeoutSeconds);
        text.requestTimeoutSeconds = value.value("requestTimeoutSeconds", text.requestTimeoutSeconds);
    }
    if (text.provider != "mock" && text.provider != "deepseek") throw ValidationError("创作助手服务商无效");
    normalizeTextEndpoint(text.baseUrl);
    if (text.model.empty() || text.model.size() > 160 || std::any_of(text.model.begin(), text.model.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return c <= 32 || c == 127; })) throw ValidationError("模型名称不能为空、过长或包含空白字符");
    if (text.connectTimeoutSeconds < 1 || text.connectTimeoutSeconds > 30 || text.requestTimeoutSeconds < 2 || text.requestTimeoutSeconds > 180 || text.connectTimeoutSeconds > text.requestTimeoutSeconds) throw ValidationError("连接超时应为 1～30 秒，响应超时应为 2～180 秒，且不能小于连接超时");
    if (patch.contains("music")) {
        const auto& value = patch.at("music");
        if (!value.is_object()) throw ValidationError("音乐模型设置必须是对象");
        for (const auto* field : {"provider", "model", "outputFormat", "previewFirst", "songapiModel"}) if (value.contains(field)) music[field] = value[field];
    }
    if (music["provider"] != "mock" && music["provider"] != "lyria" && music["provider"] != "songapi" && music["provider"] != "elevenlabs") throw ValidationError("音乐服务商选项无效");
    if (music["model"] != "lyria-3-clip-preview" && music["model"] != "lyria-3.5") throw ValidationError("音乐模型选项无效");
    if (!music["songapiModel"].is_string() || !std::set<std::string>{"v3.5", "v4", "v4.5", "v4.5-plus", "v4.5-all", "v5", "v5.5"}.contains(music["songapiModel"].get<std::string>())) throw ValidationError("SongAPI 模型选项无效");
    if (music["outputFormat"] != "wav" && music["outputFormat"] != "mp3") throw ValidationError("输出格式必须是 WAV 或 MP3");
    if (!music["previewFirst"].is_boolean()) throw ValidationError("试听优先必须是布尔值");
}
void atomicWrite(const std::filesystem::path& directory, const json& value) {
    auto temporary = directory / (uuid() + ".tmp");
    const auto payload = value.dump(2);
    int descriptor = open(temporary.c_str(), O_CREAT | O_EXCL | O_WRONLY | O_CLOEXEC | O_NOFOLLOW, 0600);
    if (descriptor < 0) throw std::runtime_error("无法保存模型设置");
    try {
        size_t offset = 0;
        while (offset < payload.size()) {
            auto count = write(descriptor, payload.data() + offset, payload.size() - offset);
            if (count < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
            if (count <= 0) throw std::runtime_error("无法保存模型设置");
            offset += static_cast<size_t>(count);
        }
        if (fsync(descriptor) != 0) throw std::runtime_error("无法保存模型设置");
        close(descriptor); descriptor = -1;
        std::filesystem::rename(temporary, directory / "settings.json");
    } catch (...) {
        if (descriptor >= 0) close(descriptor);
        std::error_code error; std::filesystem::remove(temporary, error); throw;
    }
}
}
SettingsService::SettingsService(std::filesystem::path directory) : directory_(std::move(directory)) {
    if (const auto* value = std::getenv("DEEPSEEK_API_KEY")) textEnvironmentKey_ = value;
    if (const auto* value = std::getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")) musicEnvironmentKey_ = value;
    if (const auto* value = std::getenv("SONGAPI_KEY")) songapiEnvironmentKey_ = value;
    if (const auto* value = std::getenv("ELEVENLABS_API_KEY")) elevenlabsEnvironmentKey_ = value;
    const auto file = directory_ / "settings.json";
    if (std::filesystem::exists(file)) {
        try {
            if (std::filesystem::file_size(file) > 16384) throw ValidationError("设置文件过大");
            std::ifstream input(file); auto value = json::parse(input);
            applyPreferences(text_, music_, value); // API Key properties on disk are intentionally ignored.
        } catch (...) { throw std::runtime_error("模型设置文件无效，请检查 XDG 配置目录中的 settings.json"); }
    }
}
json SettingsService::publicLocked() const {
    auto source = [](const std::string& session, const std::string& environment, bool suppressed) { return !session.empty() ? "session" : (!suppressed && !environment.empty() ? "environment" : "none"); };
    const std::string textSource = source(text_.apiKey, textEnvironmentKey_, suppressTextEnvironment_);
    const bool songapi = music_["provider"] == "songapi";
    const std::string musicSource = music_["provider"] == "elevenlabs" ? source(elevenlabsSessionKey_, elevenlabsEnvironmentKey_, suppressElevenlabsEnvironment_) : songapi ? source(songapiSessionKey_, songapiEnvironmentKey_, suppressSongapiEnvironment_) : source(musicSessionKey_, musicEnvironmentKey_, suppressMusicEnvironment_);
    auto result = persisted(text_, music_);
    result["mode"] = text_.provider == "mock" && music_["provider"] == "mock" ? "mock" : (text_.provider != "mock" && music_["provider"] != "mock" ? "live" : "mixed");
    result["text"]["endpoint"] = normalizeTextEndpoint(text_.baseUrl);
    result["text"]["hasApiKey"] = textSource != "none"; result["text"]["keySource"] = textSource;
    result["text"]["status"] = status_; result["text"]["message"] = message_;
    result["music"]["activeModel"] = music_["provider"] == "mock" ? json("MockMusicProvider") : (music_["provider"] == "elevenlabs" ? json("music_v1") : songapi ? music_["songapiModel"] : music_["model"]);
    result["music"]["activeOutputFormat"] = music_["outputFormat"]; result["music"]["status"] = musicStatus_;
    result["music"]["message"] = musicMessage_;
    result["music"]["hasApiKey"] = musicSource != "none"; result["music"]["keySource"] = musicSource;
    const auto songapiKey = !songapiSessionKey_.empty() ? songapiSessionKey_ : (suppressSongapiEnvironment_ ? "" : songapiEnvironmentKey_);
    result["music"]["songapiSandbox"] = songapiKey.starts_with("sk_test_");
    result["music"]["songapiKeySource"] = source(songapiSessionKey_, songapiEnvironmentKey_, suppressSongapiEnvironment_);
    result["music"]["elevenlabsKeySource"] = source(elevenlabsSessionKey_, elevenlabsEnvironmentKey_, suppressElevenlabsEnvironment_);
    result["music"]["lyriaKeySource"] = source(musicSessionKey_, musicEnvironmentKey_, suppressMusicEnvironment_);
    result["capabilities"] = {{"realTextProvider", true}, {"realMusicProvider", true}, {"strictDuration", true}, {"branching", true}};
    return result;
}
json SettingsService::publicSettings() { std::lock_guard lock(mutex_); return publicLocked(); }
TextSettings SettingsService::textSnapshot() {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_); auto result = text_;
    if (result.apiKey.empty() && !suppressTextEnvironment_) result.apiKey = textEnvironmentKey_;
    return result;
}
MusicSettings SettingsService::musicSnapshot() {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    if (music_["provider"] == "elevenlabs") return {"elevenlabs", "music_v1", music_["outputFormat"],
        !elevenlabsSessionKey_.empty() ? elevenlabsSessionKey_ : (suppressElevenlabsEnvironment_ ? "" : elevenlabsEnvironmentKey_), musicRevision_};
    if (music_["provider"] == "songapi") return {"songapi", music_["songapiModel"], music_["outputFormat"],
        !songapiSessionKey_.empty() ? songapiSessionKey_ : (suppressSongapiEnvironment_ ? "" : songapiEnvironmentKey_), musicRevision_};
    return {music_["provider"], music_["model"], music_["outputFormat"],
        !musicSessionKey_.empty() ? musicSessionKey_ : (suppressMusicEnvironment_ ? "" : musicEnvironmentKey_), musicRevision_};
}
void SettingsService::recordMusicTest(unsigned long revision, const TestResult& result) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    if (revision != musicRevision_) return;
    musicStatus_ = result.ok ? "connected" : "error"; musicMessage_ = result.message;
}
void SettingsService::recordTextTest(unsigned long revision, const TestResult& result) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    if (revision != text_.revision) return;
    status_ = result.ok ? "connected" : "error"; message_ = result.message;
}
json SettingsService::update(const json& patch) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    auto text = text_; auto music = music_; auto musicKey = musicSessionKey_; auto songapiKey = songapiSessionKey_;
    auto elevenlabsKey = elevenlabsSessionKey_;
    bool elevenlabsSuppressed = suppressElevenlabsEnvironment_;
    bool songapiSuppressed = suppressSongapiEnvironment_;
    bool textSuppressed = suppressTextEnvironment_, musicSuppressed = suppressMusicEnvironment_;
    applyPreferences(text, music, patch);
    auto setKey = [](const json& value, std::string& key, bool& suppressed) {
        if (value.value("clearApiKey", false)) { key.clear(); suppressed = true; }
        if (value.value("useEnvironmentKey", false)) { key.clear(); suppressed = false; }
        if (value.contains("apiKey")) { key = value.at("apiKey").get<std::string>(); validKey(key); suppressed = key.empty(); }
    };
    if (patch.contains("text")) setKey(patch["text"], text.apiKey, textSuppressed);
    if (patch.contains("music")) {
        if (music["provider"] == "elevenlabs") setKey(patch["music"], elevenlabsKey, elevenlabsSuppressed);
        else if (music["provider"] == "songapi") setKey(patch["music"], songapiKey, songapiSuppressed);
        else setKey(patch["music"], musicKey, musicSuppressed);
    }
    // Persist only non-secret preferences; apply memory keys only after the atomic write succeeds.
    atomicWrite(directory_, persisted(text, music));
    if (patch.contains("text")) { ++text.revision; status_ = "untested"; message_.clear(); }
    if (patch.contains("music")) { ++musicRevision_; musicStatus_ = "untested"; musicMessage_.clear(); }
    text_ = std::move(text); music_ = std::move(music); musicSessionKey_ = std::move(musicKey);
    elevenlabsSessionKey_ = std::move(elevenlabsKey); suppressElevenlabsEnvironment_ = elevenlabsSuppressed;
    songapiSessionKey_ = std::move(songapiKey); suppressSongapiEnvironment_ = songapiSuppressed;
    suppressTextEnvironment_ = textSuppressed; suppressMusicEnvironment_ = musicSuppressed;
    return publicLocked();
}
}
backend/src/services/text_jobs.cpp （41 行）
#include "museflow/text_jobs.hpp"
#include "museflow/runtime.hpp"
#include "museflow/database.hpp"
namespace museflow {
TextJobs::TextJobs() : worker_([this] { run(); }) {}
TextJobs::~TextJobs() { stopping_ = true; condition_.notify_all(); worker_.join(); }
json TextJobs::submit(Work work) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    if (stopping_ || pending_.size() >= 8) throw ProviderError("queue_full", "文本任务队列已满，请稍后重试", 429);
    const auto id = uuid();
    json job = {{"id", id}, {"status", "queued"}, {"createdAt", timestamp()}};
    jobs_[id] = job; pending_.emplace_back(id, std::move(work)); condition_.notify_one(); return job;
}
int TextJobs::activeCount() {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_); int count = 0;
    for (const auto& [id, job] : jobs_) if (job["status"] == "queued" || job["status"] == "running") ++count;
    return count;
}
json TextJobs::get(const std::string& id) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_); auto it = jobs_.find(id);
    if (it == jobs_.end()) throw NotFound("文本任务不存在、已过期或程序已重启，请重新发起");
    return it->second;
}
void TextJobs::run() {
    for (;;) {
        std::pair<std::string, Work> item;
        { std::unique_lock lock(mutex_); condition_.wait(lock, [this] { return stopping_ || !pending_.empty(); });
          if (stopping_) return;
          item = std::move(pending_.front()); pending_.pop_front(); jobs_[item.first]["status"] = "running"; }
        json outcome;
        try { outcome = {{"status", "completed"}, {"result", item.second(stopping_)}}; }
        catch (const ProviderError& error) { outcome = {{"status", "failed"}, {"error", error.what()}, {"code", error.code}}; }
        catch (const ValidationError& error) { outcome = {{"status", "failed"}, {"error", error.what()}, {"code", "validation"}}; }
        catch (...) { outcome = {{"status", "failed"}, {"error", "文本任务处理失败，请检查模型配置后重试"}, {"code", "internal"}}; }
        item.second = {}; // Release request settings/keys immediately; only sanitized public results remain.
        { std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
          auto& job = jobs_[item.first]; job.update(outcome); job["updatedAt"] = timestamp(); finished_.push_back(item.first);
          while (finished_.size() > 64) { jobs_.erase(finished_.front()); finished_.pop_front(); } }
    }
}
}
backend/src/utils/http_client.cpp （217 行）
#include "museflow/http_client.hpp"
#include <curl/curl.h>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <memory>
#include <algorithm>

namespace museflow {
namespace {
struct CurlRuntime {
    CurlRuntime() { if (curl_global_init(CURL_GLOBAL_DEFAULT) != CURLE_OK) throw std::runtime_error("无法初始化网络库"); }
    ~CurlRuntime() { curl_global_cleanup(); }
};
void initialize() { static CurlRuntime runtime; }
std::string part(CURLU* url, CURLUPart field) {
    char* value = nullptr;
    if (curl_url_get(url, field, &value, 0) != CURLUE_OK) return "";
    std::string result(value); curl_free(value); return result;
}
struct Buffer { std::string body; bool overflow = false; size_t limit = 2 * 1024 * 1024; };
size_t receive(char* bytes, size_t size, size_t count, void* pointer) {
    auto& buffer = *static_cast<Buffer*>(pointer);
    const size_t limit = buffer.limit;
    if (size && count > (limit - buffer.body.size()) / size) { buffer.overflow = true; return 0; }
    try { buffer.body.append(bytes, size * count); return size * count; } catch (...) { return 0; }
}
int progress(void* pointer, curl_off_t, curl_off_t, curl_off_t, curl_off_t) {
    return pointer && static_cast<const std::atomic_bool*>(pointer)->load() ? 1 : 0;
}
[[noreturn]] void httpError(long status) {
    switch (status) {
        case 400: case 422: throw ProviderError("bad_request", "模型服务拒绝了请求参数，请检查模型名称及请求参数兼容性");
        case 401: throw ProviderError("unauthorized", "API Key 无效或已过期，请在模型设置中重新输入");
        case 402: throw ProviderError("insufficient_balance", "模型账户余额不足，请检查服务商账户");
        case 403: throw ProviderError("forbidden", "没有访问该模型的权限，请检查账户授权或地区限制");
        case 404: throw ProviderError("not_found", "模型或 API 地址不存在，请检查地址和模型名称");
        case 408: case 504: throw ProviderError("timeout", "模型服务响应超时，请稍后重试或调整响应超时", 504);
        case 429: throw ProviderError("rate_limited", "模型请求过于频繁或额度已用尽，请稍后重试；本次未自动重试", 429);
        default:
            if (status >= 300 && status < 400) throw ProviderError("redirect", "模型地址返回重定向；为保护密钥，请直接配置最终 API 地址");
            throw ProviderError("upstream_error", "模型服务暂时不可用，请稍后重试");
    }
}
}
std::string normalizeTextEndpoint(const std::string& address) {
    initialize();
    if (address.empty() || address.size() > 2048 || std::any_of(address.begin(), address.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return c <= 32 || c == 127; }))
        throw ValidationError("API 地址不能为空、过长或包含空白字符");
    std::unique_ptr<CURLU, decltype(&curl_url_cleanup)> url(curl_url(), curl_url_cleanup);
    if (!url || curl_url_set(url.get(), CURLUPART_URL, address.c_str(), 0) != CURLUE_OK) throw ValidationError("API 地址不是有效的 HTTP(S) URL");
    auto scheme = part(url.get(), CURLUPART_SCHEME), host = part(url.get(), CURLUPART_HOST);
    if (scheme != "https" && scheme != "http") throw ValidationError("API 地址仅支持 HTTP(S)");
    if (scheme == "http" && host != "127.0.0.1" && host != "localhost" && host != "[::1]") throw ValidationError("远程 API 必须使用 HTTPS；HTTP 仅用于本机兼容服务");
    if (!part(url.get(), CURLUPART_USER).empty() || !part(url.get(), CURLUPART_PASSWORD).empty() || !part(url.get(), CURLUPART_QUERY).empty() || !part(url.get(), CURLUPART_FRAGMENT).empty())
        throw ValidationError("API 地址不能包含账号、密码、查询参数或片段；Key 请填入独立字段");
    auto normalized = part(url.get(), CURLUPART_URL);
    while (normalized.ends_with('/')) normalized.pop_back();
    if (!normalized.ends_with("/chat/completions")) normalized += "/chat/completions";
    return normalized;
}
json parseProviderJson(const std::string& input) {
    return json::parse(input, [](int depth, json::parse_event_t, json&) {
        if (depth > 64) throw ProviderError("invalid_response", "模型 JSON 嵌套过深，已拒绝处理");
        return true;
    });
}
bool containsSecret(const json& value, const std::string& secret) {
    if (secret.empty()) return false;
    if (value.is_string()) return value.get_ref<const std::string&>().find(secret) != std::string::npos;
    if (value.is_object() || value.is_array()) {
        for (auto it = value.begin(); it != value.end(); ++it) {
            if (value.is_object() && it.key().find(secret) != std::string::npos) return true;
            if (containsSecret(it.value(), secret)) return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}
static std::string requestBody(const std::string& url, const std::string& key, const json* body, const HttpOptions& options, bool google, bool audio = false, bool eleven = false) {
    initialize();
    if (!audio && key.empty()) throw ProviderError("missing_key", "请先在模型设置输入 API Key，或设置服务商环境变量", 400);
    if (key.size() > 8192 || std::any_of(key.begin(), key.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return c < 33 || c > 126; })) throw ValidationError("API Key 格式无效");
    if (options.cancelled && options.cancelled->load()) throw ProviderError("cancelled", "服务正在关闭，模型请求已取消", 503);
    std::unique_ptr<CURL, decltype(&curl_easy_cleanup)> handle(curl_easy_init(), curl_easy_cleanup);
    if (!handle) throw ProviderError("network_init", "无法初始化模型网络请求");
    curl_slist* rawHeaders = nullptr;
    std::vector<std::string> requestHeaders;
    if (!audio) requestHeaders = {"Content-Type: application/json", (eleven ? "Accept: audio/mpeg, application/json" : "Accept: application/json"), (eleven ? "xi-api-key: " : google ? "x-goog-api-key: " : "Authorization: Bearer ") + key};
    for (const auto& header : requestHeaders) {
        auto* next = curl_slist_append(rawHeaders, header.c_str());
        if (!next) { curl_slist_free_all(rawHeaders); throw std::bad_alloc(); }
        rawHeaders = next;
    }
    std::unique_ptr<curl_slist, decltype(&curl_slist_free_all)> headers(rawHeaders, curl_slist_free_all);
    const auto payload = body ? body->dump() : ""; Buffer buffer;
    if (audio || eleven) buffer.limit = 128 * 1024 * 1024;
    if (google) buffer.limit = 192 * 1024 * 1024;
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_URL, url.c_str());
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, headers.get());
    if (body) {
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_POST, 1L);
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, payload.c_str());
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_POSTFIELDSIZE_LARGE, static_cast<curl_off_t>(payload.size()));
    }
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT, static_cast<long>(options.connectTimeoutSeconds));
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_TIMEOUT, static_cast<long>(options.requestTimeoutSeconds));
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_NOSIGNAL, 1L);
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_FOLLOWLOCATION, 0L);
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER, 1L);
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYHOST, 2L);
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION, receive);
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_WRITEDATA, &buffer);
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_NOPROGRESS, 0L);
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_XFERINFOFUNCTION, progress);
    curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_XFERINFODATA, options.cancelled);
    // libcurl reads HTTPS_PROXY and NO_PROXY itself. Standalone Linux app also accepts HTTP_PROXY.
    if (url.starts_with("http:") && !std::getenv("http_proxy")) {
        if (const auto* proxy = std::getenv("HTTP_PROXY")) curl_easy_setopt(handle.get(), CURLOPT_PROXY, proxy);
    }
    auto result = curl_easy_perform(handle.get());
    if (result != CURLE_OK) {
        if (buffer.overflow) throw ProviderError("response_too_large", "模型响应超过本地大小限制，已停止接收");
        if (result == CURLE_OPERATION_TIMEDOUT) throw ProviderError("timeout", "连接或模型响应超时，请检查网络、代理或调整响应超时", 504);
        if (result == CURLE_ABORTED_BY_CALLBACK) throw ProviderError("cancelled", "服务正在关闭，模型请求已取消", 503);
        if (result == CURLE_COULDNT_RESOLVE_HOST || result == CURLE_COULDNT_RESOLVE_PROXY) throw ProviderError("dns_error", "无法解析模型或代理地址，请检查网络及代理设置");
        if (result == CURLE_PEER_FAILED_VERIFICATION || result == CURLE_SSL_CONNECT_ERROR) throw ProviderError("tls_error", "模型 HTTPS 证书或安全连接验证失败，请检查系统时间和证书");
        throw ProviderError("network_error", "无法连接模型服务，请检查网络、地址及代理设置");
    }
    long status = 0; curl_easy_getinfo(handle.get(), CURLINFO_RESPONSE_CODE, &status);
    if (eleven && (status < 200 || status >= 300)) {
        // Inspect only known codes. Never expose raw upstream messages or credentials.
        std::string code, detailStatus;
        try {
            const auto error = parseProviderJson(buffer.body).value("detail", json::object());
            if (error.is_object()) { code = error.value("code", ""); detailStatus = error.value("status", ""); }
        } catch (...) {}
        if (code == "paid_plan_required") throw ProviderError("paid_plan_required", "ElevenLabs 音乐 API 需要付费套餐，请检查订阅是否已生效");
        if (detailStatus == "missing_permissions" || code == "missing_permissions") throw ProviderError("missing_permissions", "ElevenLabs Key 缺少音乐接口权限，请在控制台启用 Music 权限");
        if (status == 402 || code == "quota_exceeded" || detailStatus == "quota_exceeded") throw ProviderError("insufficient_balance", "ElevenLabs 音乐额度不足，请检查账户及 Key 的额度限制");
    }
    if (status < 200 || status >= 300) httpError(status);
    if (eleven) {
        if (buffer.body.find(key) != std::string::npos) throw ProviderError("unsafe_response", "模型响应包含敏感凭据，已丢弃响应");
        char* contentType = nullptr;
        curl_easy_getinfo(handle.get(), CURLINFO_CONTENT_TYPE, &contentType);
        const std::string type = contentType ? contentType : "";
        const bool plan = url.ends_with("/music/plan");
        if (plan ? !type.starts_with("application/json") : !(type.starts_with("audio/mpeg") || type.starts_with("audio/mp3") || type.starts_with("application/octet-stream")))
            throw ProviderError("invalid_response", "ElevenLabs 返回了不支持的响应类型");
    }
    return buffer.body;
}
static json requestJson(const std::string& url, const std::string& key, const json* body, const HttpOptions& options, bool google) {
    const auto content = requestBody(url, key, body, options, google);
    // Never return raw upstream errors: they may echo Authorization headers or submitted keys.
    if (content.find(key) != std::string::npos) throw ProviderError("unsafe_response", "模型响应包含敏感凭据，已丢弃响应");
    try {
        auto value = parseProviderJson(content);
        if (containsSecret(value, key)) throw ProviderError("unsafe_response", "模型响应包含敏感凭据，已丢弃响应");
        return value;
    }
    catch (const json::exception&) { throw ProviderError("invalid_response", "模型服务返回的内容不是有效 JSON，请检查 API 兼容性"); }
}
json postModelJson(const std::string& endpoint, const std::string& key, const json& body, const HttpOptions& options) {
    return requestJson(normalizeTextEndpoint(endpoint), key, &body, options, false);
}
json requestGoogleJson(const std::string& path, const std::string& key, const json* body, const HttpOptions& options) {
    std::string base = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta";
    // Test injection is restricted to loopback; production always uses Google's official endpoint.
    if (const char* fixture = std::getenv("MUSEFLOW_GOOGLE_TEST_BASE")) {
        std::string value = fixture;
        auto normalized = normalizeTextEndpoint(value);
        if (!normalized.starts_with("http://127.0.0.1:")) throw ValidationError("Google 测试地址仅允许本机 IPv4 回环地址");
        base = normalized.substr(0, normalized.size() - std::string("/chat/completions").size());
    }
    return requestJson(base + path, key, body, options, true);
}
namespace {
std::string songApiBase() {
    if (const auto* fixture = std::getenv("MUSEFLOW_SONGAPI_TEST_BASE")) {
        const auto normalized = normalizeTextEndpoint(fixture);
        if (!normalized.starts_with("http://127.0.0.1:")) throw ValidationError("SongAPI 测试地址仅允许本机 IPv4 回环地址");
        return normalized.substr(0, normalized.size() - std::string("/chat/completions").size());
    }
    return "https://api.songapi.dev";
}
}
std::string requestElevenLabs(const std::string& path, const std::string& key, const json& body, const HttpOptions& options) {
    std::string base = "https://api.elevenlabs.io";
    if (const auto* fixture = std::getenv("MUSEFLOW_ELEVENLABS_TEST_BASE")) {
        const auto normalized = normalizeTextEndpoint(fixture);
        if (!normalized.starts_with("http://127.0.0.1:")) throw ValidationError("ElevenLabs 测试地址仅允许本机 IPv4 回环地址");
        base = normalized.substr(0, normalized.size() - std::string("/chat/completions").size());
    }
    return requestBody(base + path, key, &body, options, false, false, true);
}
json requestSongApiJson(const std::string& path, const std::string& key, const json* body, const HttpOptions& options) {
    return requestJson(songApiBase() + path, key, body, options, false);
}
std::string downloadSongApiAudio(const std::string& address, const HttpOptions& options) {
    initialize();
    // Audio is fetched without credentials; only known audio hosts (or the explicit fixture) are accepted.
    if (address.empty() || address.size() > 8192 || std::any_of(address.begin(), address.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return c <= 32 || c == 127; }))
        throw ProviderError("invalid_audio_url", "SongAPI 返回了无效音频地址");
    std::unique_ptr<CURLU, decltype(&curl_url_cleanup)> url(curl_url(), curl_url_cleanup);
    if (!url || curl_url_set(url.get(), CURLUPART_URL, address.c_str(), 0) != CURLUE_OK)
        throw ProviderError("invalid_audio_url", "SongAPI 返回了无效音频地址");
    const auto host = part(url.get(), CURLUPART_HOST);
    const auto base = songApiBase();
    const bool fixture = base.starts_with("http://127.0.0.1:") && address.starts_with(base + "/");
    const auto port = part(url.get(), CURLUPART_PORT);
    const bool allowed = part(url.get(), CURLUPART_SCHEME) == "https" && (port.empty() || port == "443") &&
        (host == "cdn.songapi.dev" || host == "cdn.suno.ai" || host == "cdn1.suno.ai" || host == "cdn2.suno.ai" || host == "cdn.suno.com");
    if ((!fixture && !allowed) || !part(url.get(), CURLUPART_USER).empty() || !part(url.get(), CURLUPART_PASSWORD).empty() || !part(url.get(), CURLUPART_FRAGMENT).empty())
        throw ProviderError("invalid_audio_url", "SongAPI 返回了未支持的音频下载地址，请在 SongAPI 控制台查看结果");
    return requestBody(address, "", nullptr, options, false, true);
}

backend/src/utils/runtime.cpp （72 行）
#include "museflow/runtime.hpp"
#include <array>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <ctime>
#include <iomanip>
#include <sstream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <sys/random.h>
#include <cerrno>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/file.h>
#include <unistd.h>

namespace museflow {
DataDirectoryLock::DataDirectoryLock(const std::filesystem::path& directory) {
    descriptor_ = open((directory / ".instance.lock").c_str(), O_CREAT | O_RDWR | O_CLOEXEC | O_NOFOLLOW, 0600);
    if (descriptor_ < 0) throw std::runtime_error("无法锁定数据目录，请检查目录权限");
    if (flock(descriptor_, LOCK_EX | LOCK_NB) != 0) {
        close(descriptor_); descriptor_ = -1;
        throw std::runtime_error("此数据目录已有 MuseFlow 实例运行，请勿重复启动");
    }
}
DataDirectoryLock::~DataDirectoryLock() { if (descriptor_ >= 0) close(descriptor_); }
std::string uuid() {
    std::array<unsigned char, 16> bytes{};
    std::size_t offset = 0;
    while (offset < bytes.size()) {
        auto count = getrandom(bytes.data() + offset, bytes.size() - offset, 0);
        if (count < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
        if (count <= 0) throw std::runtime_error("无法生成安全标识符");
        offset += static_cast<std::size_t>(count);
    }
    bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 0x0f) | 0x40;
    bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80;
    std::ostringstream out;
    out << std::hex << std::setfill('0');
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < bytes.size(); ++i) {
        if (i == 4 || i == 6 || i == 8 || i == 10) out << '-';
        out << std::setw(2) << static_cast<int>(bytes[i]);
    }
    return out.str();
}
bool isUuid(const std::string& value) {
    if (value.size() != 36) return false;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < value.size(); ++i) {
        if (i == 8 || i == 13 || i == 18 || i == 23) { if (value[i] != '-') return false; }
        else if (!((value[i] >= '0' && value[i] <= '9') || (value[i] >= 'a' && value[i] <= 'f'))) return false;
    }
    return true;
}
std::string timestamp() {
    auto now = std::time(nullptr);
    std::tm value{}; gmtime_r(&now, &value);
    std::ostringstream out; out << std::put_time(&value, "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ");
    return out.str();
}
RuntimePaths RuntimePaths::create() {
    const auto* home = std::getenv("HOME");
    if (!home) throw std::runtime_error("未设置 HOME");
    auto path = [home](const char* variable, const char* fallback) {
        const auto* value = std::getenv(variable);
        auto base = value && std::filesystem::path(value).is_absolute() ? std::filesystem::path(value) : std::filesystem::path(home) / fallback;
        return base / "museflow";
    };
    RuntimePaths result{path("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", ".config"), path("XDG_DATA_HOME", ".local/share"), path("XDG_CACHE_HOME", ".cache")};
    for (const auto& directory : {result.config, result.data, result.cache, result.data / "projects"}) {
        std::filesystem::create_directories(directory);
        std::filesystem::permissions(directory, std::filesystem::perms::owner_all, std::filesystem::perm_options::replace);
    }
    return result;
}

backend/tests/core_tests.cpp （174 行）
#include "museflow/generation_queue.hpp"
#include "museflow/runtime.hpp"
#include "museflow/schema.hpp"
#include "museflow/settings.hpp"
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <set>

using namespace museflow;
static int checks = 0;
void check(bool condition, const std::string& name) {
    if (!condition) throw std::runtime_error("FAIL: " + name);
    ++checks;
}
template<class F> void invalid(F function, const std::string& name) {
    try { function(); } catch (const ValidationError&) { ++checks; return; }
    throw std::runtime_error("FAIL: accepted " + name);
}
int main() {
    const auto root = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / ("museflow-test-" + uuid());
    std::filesystem::create_directories(root);
    try {
        auto b = MockTextProvider{}.createBlueprint("旅人走进森林，情绪逐渐明亮");
        check(parseBlueprint(json(b)).title == b.title, "blueprint roundtrip");
        check(isUuid(b.id) && !isUuid("../../secret"), "UUID path guard");
        check(b.finalLyriaPrompt.find("0s–15s") != std::string::npos, "timed prompt");
        auto bad = b; bad.structure[1].startSeconds = 14;
        invalid([&] { validate(bad); }, "overlapping sections");
        bad = b; bad.structure[0].endSeconds = 0;
        invalid([&] { validate(bad); }, "empty section");
        bad = b; bad.structure.back().endSeconds = 61;
        invalid([&] { validate(bad); }, "section past target");
        bad = b; bad.structure[0].intensity = 11;
        invalid([&] { validate(bad); }, "intensity");
        bad = b; bad.bpm = 0;
        invalid([&] { validate(bad); }, "bpm");
        bad = b; bad.targetDurationSeconds = 601;
        invalid([&] { validate(bad); }, "duration");
        bad = b; bad.structure[1].id = bad.structure[0].id;
        invalid([&] { validate(bad); }, "duplicate section id");
        invalid([&] { parseBlueprint(json{{"title", "incomplete"}}); }, "missing required fields");
        json broken = b; broken["bpm"] = "fast";
        invalid([&] { parseBlueprint(broken); }, "wrong JSON type");
        broken = b; broken["structure"][0]["intensity"] = 1.5;
        invalid([&] { parseBlueprint(broken); }, "fractional intensity");
        broken = b; broken["bpm"] = 4294967384ULL;
        invalid([&] { parseBlueprint(broken); }, "integer overflow before bpm conversion");
        broken = b; broken["structure"][0]["intensity"] = 4294967299ULL;
        invalid([&] { parseBlueprint(broken); }, "integer overflow before intensity conversion");
        check(containsSecret(json{{"nested", json::array({"escaped-secret"})}}, "escaped-secret"), "decoded secret inspection");
        json extra = b; extra["apiKey"] = "test-not-a-key";
        check(!json(parseBlueprint(extra)).contains("apiKey"), "unknown secrets excluded");
        json modelOutput = b; modelOutput.erase("promptSource"); modelOutput.erase("promptFingerprint");
        modelOutput["finalLyriaPrompt"] = "AI-authored cinematic direction";
        auto generated = parseModelBlueprint(modelOutput);
        check(generated.promptSource == "deepseek" && promptIsCurrent(generated), "model prompt freshness");
        check(parseBlueprint(json(generated)).finalLyriaPrompt == "AI-authored cinematic direction", "preserve unedited AI prompt");
        generated.bpm = 105;
        check(parseBlueprint(json(generated)).finalLyriaPrompt.find("105 BPM") != std::string::npos, "manual changes regenerate stale prompt");
        auto invalidOutput = modelOutput; invalidOutput["extra-field"] = "ignored?";
        invalid([&] { parseModelBlueprint(invalidOutput); }, "schema additional property");
        invalidOutput = modelOutput; invalidOutput.erase("finalLyriaPrompt");
        invalid([&] { parseModelBlueprint(invalidOutput); }, "schema missing final prompt");
        invalidOutput = modelOutput; invalidOutput["strictDuration"] = "false";
        invalid([&] { parseModelBlueprint(invalidOutput); }, "schema bool type");
        invalidOutput = modelOutput; invalidOutput["lyrics"] = nullptr;
        invalid([&] { parseModelBlueprint(invalidOutput); }, "schema null string");
        check(normalizeTextEndpoint("https://api.example.com/v1/") == "https://api.example.com/v1/chat/completions", "base endpoint normalization");
        check(normalizeTextEndpoint("http://127.0.0.1:19876/v1/chat/completions") == "http://127.0.0.1:19876/v1/chat/completions", "full local endpoint");
        invalid([&] { normalizeTextEndpoint("http://remote.example/v1"); }, "remote plaintext endpoint");
        invalid([&] { normalizeTextEndpoint("https://name:password@example.com/v1"); }, "URL credentials");
        invalid([&] { normalizeTextEndpoint("https://example.com/v1?api_key=example"); }, "URL query keys");
        invalid([&] { normalizeTextEndpoint("file:///etc/passwd"); }, "local file protocol");
        setenv("DEEPSEEK_API_KEY", "fixture-environment-text", 1); setenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "fixture-environment-music", 1);
        auto config = root / "config"; std::filesystem::create_directory(config);
        {
            SettingsService settings(config);
            check(settings.publicSettings()["text"]["keySource"] == "environment", "environment text key source");
            check(settings.publicSettings()["music"]["keySource"] == "environment", "environment music key source");
            auto saved = settings.update({{"text", {{"apiKey", "fixture-session-text"}, {"model", "custom-model"}}}, {"music", {{"apiKey", "fixture-session-music"}, {"outputFormat", "mp3"}}}});
            check(settings.textSnapshot().apiKey == "fixture-session-text", "session overrides environment");
            check(saved.dump().find("fixture-session") == std::string::npos, "public settings redact session keys");
            check(settings.musicSnapshot().apiKey == "fixture-session-music", "music session snapshot");
            auto musicSnapshot = settings.musicSnapshot();
            settings.update({{"music", {{"provider", "lyria"}}}});
            settings.recordMusicTest(musicSnapshot.revision, {true, "stale"});
            check(settings.publicSettings()["music"]["status"] == "untested", "stale music test ignored");
            check(settings.musicSnapshot().provider == "lyria" && settings.publicSettings()["mode"] == "mixed", "independent music provider");
            settings.update({{"music", {{"clearApiKey", true}}}});
            check(settings.musicSnapshot().apiKey.empty(), "music clear suppresses environment");
            settings.update({{"music", {{"useEnvironmentKey", true}}}});
            check(settings.musicSnapshot().apiKey == "fixture-environment-music", "restore music environment");
            invalid([&] { settings.update({{"music", {{"provider", "chat"}}}}); }, "reject generic music chat provider");
            auto snapshot = settings.textSnapshot();
            settings.update({{"text", {{"model", "new-model"}}}});
            settings.recordTextTest(snapshot.revision, {true, "stale"});
            check(settings.publicSettings()["text"]["status"] == "untested", "stale connection test ignored");
            settings.update({{"text", {{"clearApiKey", true}}}});
            check(settings.textSnapshot().apiKey.empty(), "clear also suppresses environment");
            settings.update({{"text", {{"useEnvironmentKey", true}}}});
            check(settings.textSnapshot().apiKey == "fixture-environment-text", "restore environment");
            invalid([&] { settings.update({{"text", {{"apiKey", "bad\nkey"}}}}); }, "header injection in key");
            invalid([&] { settings.update({{"text", {{"requestTimeoutSeconds", 0}}}}); }, "invalid timeout");
            std::ifstream stream(config / "settings.json"); std::string savedFile((std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(stream)), {});
            check(savedFile.find("fixture-") == std::string::npos && savedFile.find("apiKey") == std::string::npos, "no key persisted to config");
        }
        unsetenv("DEEPSEEK_API_KEY"); unsetenv("GEMINI_API_KEY");
        {
            SettingsService restarted(config);
            check(!restarted.publicSettings()["text"]["hasApiKey"].get<bool>() && !restarted.publicSettings()["music"]["hasApiKey"].get<bool>(), "session keys vanish after restart");
            check(restarted.publicSettings()["text"]["model"] == "new-model", "nonsecret preferences survive restart");
        }
        std::string projectId, firstTaskId;
        {
            Database db(root / "test.db");
            auto project = db.saveProject({{"name", "quote ' ; --"}, {"blueprint", b}, {"apiKey", "test-not-a-key"}});
            projectId = project["id"];
            check(db.projects().size() == 1 && db.project(projectId)["name"] == "quote ' ; --", "bound SQL parameters");
            check(!db.project(projectId).contains("apiKey"), "project property whitelist");
            {
                GenerationQueue queue(db, root, std::make_unique<MockMusicProvider>(std::filesystem::path(MUSEFLOW_ASSET_DIR) / "mock-demo.wav"));
                auto first = queue.enqueue(projectId, b, "preview"); firstTaskId = first["id"];
                check(first["status"] == "queued", "initial queued state");
                invalid([&] { db.deleteProject(projectId); }, "delete busy project");

                invalid([&] { queue.enqueue(projectId, b, "invented"); }, "invalid generation mode");
                b.bpm = 100;
                auto second = queue.enqueue(projectId, b, "full");
                std::set<std::string> states;
                auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(12);
                while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline) {
                    states.insert(db.task(firstTaskId)["status"]);
                    if (db.task(second["id"])["status"] == "completed") break;
                    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(30));
                }
                check(db.versions(projectId).size() == 2, "two immutable versions");
                for (const auto* state : {"preparing", "generating", "post_processing", "completed"}) check(states.contains(state), std::string("observed state ") + state);
                auto versions = db.versions(projectId);
                check(versions[0]["blueprint"]["bpm"] == 100 && versions[1]["blueprint"]["bpm"] == 88, "version snapshots preserved");
                check(db.task(firstTaskId)["modelTargetDurationSeconds"] == 30 && db.task(firstTaskId)["actualDurationSeconds"] == 8, "target versus actual duration");
                for (const auto& version : versions) check(std::filesystem::file_size(root / "projects" / projectId / (version["generationId"].get<std::string>() + ".wav")) > 44, "audio written");
            }
            auto parent = db.versions(projectId).back();
            auto branch = db.branchVersion(parent["id"]);
            check(branch["draftParentVersionId"] == parent["id"] && branch["blueprint"]["id"] != parent["blueprint"]["id"], "branch creates a persistent draft with fresh blueprint id");
            check(db.versions(projectId).size() == 2, "branch does not fabricate a generated version");
            auto metadata = db.updateVersionMetadata(parent["id"], {{"rating", 4}, {"notes", "更适合片尾"}});
            check(metadata["rating"] == 4 && metadata["blueprint"] == parent["blueprint"], "metadata preserves blueprint snapshot");
            invalid([&] { db.updateVersionMetadata(parent["id"], {{"rating", 4294967300LL}}); }, "rating integer overflow rejected");
            invalid([&] { db.updateVersionMetadata(parent["id"], {{"blueprint", b}}); }, "immutable version fields rejected");
            check(db.activeTaskCount() == 0, "completed tasks excluded from activity");
            check(db.exportProject(projectId)["versions"].size() == 2 && !db.exportProject(projectId)["includesAudio"].get<bool>(), "structured project export includes version records");
            db.createTask({{"id", "interrupted-test"}, {"projectId", projectId}, {"status", "generating"}});
        }
        {
            Database db(root / "test.db");
            check(db.projects().size() == 1 && db.versions(projectId).size() == 2, "restart persistence");
            check(db.task("interrupted-test")["status"] == "failed", "interrupted task recovery");
            check(db.task(firstTaskId)["status"] == "completed", "completed task survives restart");
            {
                GenerationQueue queue(db, root, std::make_unique<MockMusicProvider>(root / "missing.wav"));
                auto failed = queue.enqueue(projectId, b, "preview");
                for (int i = 0; i < 100 && db.task(failed["id"])["status"] != "failed"; ++i) std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(30));
                check(db.task(failed["id"])["status"] == "failed", "missing audio failure");
            }
            db.deleteProject(projectId);
            check(db.projects().empty() && db.versions().empty(), "cascade deletion");
        }
        std::filesystem::remove_all(root);
        std::cout << "Passed " << checks << " core checks\n"; return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        std::cerr << error.what() << '\n'; std::filesystem::remove_all(root); return 1;
    }
}

# cpp-httplib

官方项目：https://github.com/yhirose/cpp-httplib

本项目固定使用 `v0.20.1` 的 `httplib.h`，采用 MIT 许可证；完整许可证见 `cpp-httplib.LICENSE`。头文件直接随项目保存，CMake 构建不需要联网获取 HTTP 框架。

原始文件：https://raw.githubusercontent.com/yhirose/cpp-httplib/v0.20.1/httplib.h
