frontend/src/components/AudioPlayer.vue （51 行）
<script setup lang="ts">
import { onBeforeUnmount, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import { audioUrl } from '../api/client'
const props = defineProps<{ generationId: string; title: string; target: number; actual: number; format?: string; provider?: string; sourceDuration?: number; strict?: boolean }>()
const audio = ref<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null)
const peaks = ref<number[]>([]), progress = ref(0), error = ref('')
let controller: AbortController | undefined
let epoch = 0
watch(() => props.generationId, async id => {
  const current = ++epoch
  controller?.abort(); controller = new AbortController(); peaks.value = []; progress.value = 0; error.value = ''
  let context: AudioContext | undefined
  try {
    const response = await fetch(audioUrl(id), { signal: controller.signal })
    if (!response.ok) return
    const bytes = await response.arrayBuffer()
    context = new AudioContext()
    const buffer = await context.decodeAudioData(bytes)
    if (current !== epoch) return
    const channel = buffer.getChannelData(0)
    const step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(channel.length / 80))
    peaks.value = Array.from({ length: 80 }, (_, i) => {
      let peak = 0
      for (let j = i * step; j < Math.min(channel.length, (i + 1) * step); j++) peak = Math.max(peak, Math.abs(channel[j]!))
      return Math.max(3, peak * 90)
    })
  } catch { /* Native audio controls remain usable when waveform decoding is unavailable. */ }
  finally { if (context) await context.close().catch(() => {}) }
}, { immediate: true })
function updateProgress() { if (audio.value?.duration) progress.value = audio.value.currentTime / audio.value.duration }
function seek(event: MouseEvent) {
  if (!audio.value || !Number.isFinite(audio.value.duration)) return
  const bounds = (event.currentTarget as SVGElement).getBoundingClientRect()
  audio.value.currentTime = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (event.clientX - bounds.left) / bounds.width)) * audio.value.duration
}
function pauseOthers() { document.querySelectorAll('audio').forEach(player => { if (player !== audio.value) player.pause() }) }
onBeforeUnmount(() => { epoch++; controller?.abort(); audio.value?.pause() })
</script>
<template>
  <div class="audio-player">
    <div class="audio-title"><div class="cover-note">♫</div><div><strong>{{ title }}</strong><small>{{ provider === 'elevenlabs' ? 'ElevenLabs 音频' : provider === 'lyria' ? 'Lyria 音频' : provider === 'songapi' ? 'SongAPI 音频' : '模拟音频' }} · {{ (format ?? 'wav').toUpperCase() }} · 本机保存</small></div></div>
    <svg v-if="peaks.length" class="waveform" viewBox="0 0 480 76" aria-label="实际音频波形，点击可跳转播放位置" @click="seek">
      <rect v-for="(peak, i) in peaks" :key="i" :x="i * 6" :y="38 - peak / 2" width="3" :height="peak" rx="1.5" :fill="i / 80 < progress ? '#6255c7' : '#cec9ed'" />
    </svg>
    <p v-else class="muted">波形加载中；也可直接使用下方播放器。</p>
    <audio ref="audio" controls preload="metadata" :src="audioUrl(generationId)" @timeupdate="updateProgress" @play="pauseOthers" @error="error = '音频加载失败，请检查本地服务及音频文件'" :aria-label="`播放 ${title}`"></audio>
    <p v-if="error" class="error" role="alert">{{ error }}</p>
    <div class="duration-row"><span>模型目标时长 <b>{{ target }} 秒</b></span><span v-if="sourceDuration !== undefined">原始音频时长 <b>{{ sourceDuration.toFixed(3) }} 秒</b></span><span>{{ strict ? '处理后实际时长' : '实际音频时长' }} <b>{{ actual.toFixed(3) }} 秒</b></span></div>
    <a class="button download-button" :href="audioUrl(generationId, true)" download>↓ 下载 {{ (format ?? 'wav').toUpperCase() }}</a>
  </div>
</template>
frontend/src/components/ComparePlayer.vue （37 行）
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, nextTick, onBeforeUnmount, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import type { Version } from '../types'
import { audioUrl } from '../api/client'
import { switchPosition } from '../types/versions'
const props = defineProps<{ a: Version; b: Version }>()
const left = ref<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null), right = ref<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null)
const selected = ref<'a' | 'b'>('a'), loop = ref(false), error = ref(''), switching = ref(false)
let epoch = 0
const current = computed(() => selected.value === 'a' ? left.value : right.value)
function stop() { left.value?.pause(); right.value?.pause() }
function pauseOthers(player: HTMLAudioElement | null) { document.querySelectorAll('audio').forEach(audio => { if (audio !== player) audio.pause() }) }
async function select(side: 'a' | 'b') {
  if (switching.value) return
  const previous = current.value, position = previous?.currentTime ?? 0, playing = previous && !previous.paused
  const next = side === 'a' ? left.value : right.value
  const ticket = ++epoch
  stop(); selected.value = side; error.value = ''; switching.value = true
  try {
    if (!next) return
    if (next.readyState < 1) await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      const clean = () => { next.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', ready); next.removeEventListener('error', failed); clearTimeout(timer) }
      const ready = () => { clean(); resolve() }, failed = () => { clean(); reject(new Error('音频加载失败')) }
      const timer = setTimeout(() => { clean(); reject(new Error('音频加载超时')) }, 10000)
      next.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', ready); next.addEventListener('error', failed)
    })
    if (ticket !== epoch) return
    next.currentTime = switchPosition(position, next.duration)
    if (playing) { pauseOthers(next); await next.play() }
  } catch (e) { if (ticket === epoch) error.value = (e as Error).message }
  finally { if (ticket === epoch) switching.value = false }
}
async function play() { try { if (current.value) { pauseOthers(current.value); await current.value.play(); error.value = '' } } catch { error.value = '无法播放所选音频，请检查本地音频文件' } }
watch(() => [props.a.id, props.b.id], async () => { epoch++; stop(); selected.value = 'a'; switching.value = false; error.value = ''; await nextTick(); left.value?.load(); right.value?.load() })
onBeforeUnmount(() => { epoch++; stop() })
</script>
<template><section class="card compare-player"><div class="section-heading"><div><span class="eyebrow">LISTEN SIDE BY SIDE</span><h2>A/B 试听</h2></div><label class="checkbox"><input v-model="loop" type="checkbox" />循环播放当前版本</label></div><p class="muted">切换保留绝对播放位置；若新版本较短，则定位到其结尾附近。仅播放当前一侧。</p><div class="ab-switch"><button class="button" :class="{ primary: selected === 'a' }" :aria-pressed="selected === 'a'" :disabled="switching" @click="select('a')">切换到 A</button><button class="button" :class="{ primary: selected === 'b' }" :aria-pressed="selected === 'b'" :disabled="switching" @click="select('b')">切换到 B</button><button class="button" @click="play" :disabled="switching">播放当前版本</button><button class="text-button" @click="stop">暂停</button></div><div class="ab-tracks"><div><strong>A · {{ a.title }} <small>{{ a.id.slice(0, 8) }}</small></strong><audio ref="left" controls preload="auto" :src="audioUrl(a.generationId)" :loop="loop" :aria-label="'对比 A 音频'" @play="selected = 'a'; pauseOthers(left)" @error="error = 'A 音频加载失败'" /></div><div><strong>B · {{ b.title }} <small>{{ b.id.slice(0, 8) }}</small></strong><audio ref="right" controls preload="auto" :src="audioUrl(b.generationId)" :loop="loop" :aria-label="'对比 B 音频'" @play="selected = 'b'; pauseOthers(right)" @error="error = 'B 音频加载失败'" /></div></div><p v-if="error" role="alert" class="error">{{ error }}</p><p v-if="a.provider === 'mock' || b.provider === 'mock'" class="small-note">模拟版本使用同一原始测试音频；参数差异不会改变原始演奏，严格时长处理可能不同。</p></section></template>
frontend/src/components/TimelineEditor.vue （50 行）
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue'
import type { MusicBlueprint } from '../types'
import { splitTags } from '../types/validation'
const blueprint = defineModel<MusicBlueprint>({ required: true })
const sorted = computed(() => [...blueprint.value.structure].sort((a, b) => a.startSeconds - b.startSeconds))
function addSection() {
  const b = blueprint.value
  if (b.structure.length >= 32) return
  let start = 0, end = b.targetDurationSeconds
  for (const section of sorted.value) {
    if (section.startSeconds - start >= 1) { end = section.startSeconds; break }
    start = Math.max(start, section.endSeconds)
  }
  if (end - start < 1) {
    const longest = [...b.structure].sort((a, c) => (c.endSeconds - c.startSeconds) - (a.endSeconds - a.startSeconds))[0]
    if (!longest || longest.endSeconds <= longest.startSeconds) return
    start = Math.round((longest.startSeconds + longest.endSeconds) * 50) / 100
    end = longest.endSeconds; longest.endSeconds = start
  }
  b.structure.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: '新的段落', startSeconds: start, endSeconds: end, emotion: '平静', intensity: 5, instruments: [], description: '' })
}
</script>
<template>
  <section class="card timeline-card">
    <div class="section-heading"><div><span class="eyebrow">03 / EMOTION TIMELINE</span><h2>让情绪沿时间展开</h2></div><button class="button small" @click="addSection" :disabled="blueprint.structure.length >= 32">＋ 添加段落</button></div>
    <p class="muted">每一段都是音乐的叙事方向。可在描述中注明乐器的进入、退出和过渡。</p>
    <div class="timeline-ruler"><span>0:00</span><span>{{ blueprint.targetDurationSeconds }} 秒 · 目标</span></div>
    <div class="timeline-track" aria-label="情绪时间轴预览">
      <div v-for="(section, index) in sorted" :key="section.id" class="timeline-block" :class="`tone-${index % 3}`" :style="{ left: `${Math.max(0, section.startSeconds / blueprint.targetDurationSeconds * 100)}%`, width: `${Math.max(0, (section.endSeconds - section.startSeconds) / blueprint.targetDurationSeconds * 100)}%` }"><span>{{ section.name }}</span><small>{{ section.intensity }}/10</small></div>
    </div>
    <div v-for="(section, index) in blueprint.structure" :key="section.id" class="section-editor">
      <div class="section-number">{{ String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0') }}</div>
      <div class="section-fields">
        <div class="form-grid section-first-row">
          <label>段落名称<input v-model="section.name" maxlength="60" /></label>
          <label>开始（秒）<input v-model.number="section.startSeconds" type="number" min="0" step="0.1" /></label>
          <label>结束（秒）<input v-model.number="section.endSeconds" type="number" min="0" step="0.1" /></label>
        </div>
        <div class="form-grid three">
          <label>情绪<input v-model="section.emotion" maxlength="60" /></label>
          <label>强度 · {{ section.intensity }}/10<input v-model.number="section.intensity" type="range" min="1" max="10" /></label>
          <label>段落乐器<input :value="section.instruments.join('、')" @change="section.instruments = splitTags(($event.target as HTMLInputElement).value)" placeholder="钢琴、弦乐" /></label>
        </div>
        <label>过渡与编配描述<input v-model="section.description" placeholder="例如：弦乐渐入，鼓组在段落末尾退出" maxlength="600" /></label>
      </div>
      <button class="icon-button danger" :aria-label="`删除段落 ${index + 1}`" @click="blueprint.structure.splice(index, 1)" title="删除段落">×</button>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>
frontend/src/components/VersionMetadata.vue （15 行）
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, watch } from 'vue'
import type { Version } from '../types'
import { api } from '../api/client'
const props = defineProps<{ version: Version }>()
const emit = defineEmits<{ saved: [version: Version] }>()
const rating = ref(0), notes = ref(''), busy = ref(false), error = ref(''), saved = ref(false)
watch(() => props.version.id, () => { rating.value = props.version.rating; notes.value = props.version.notes; saved.value = false }, { immediate: true })
async function save() {
  busy.value = true; error.value = ''; saved.value = false
  try { const result = await api<Version>(`/versions/${props.version.id}/metadata`, 'PUT', { rating: rating.value, notes: notes.value }); emit('saved', result); saved.value = true }
  catch (e) { error.value = (e as Error).message } finally { busy.value = false }
}
</script>
<template><details><summary>评分与备注{{ version.rating ? ` · ${version.rating} / 5` : '' }}</summary><form @submit.prevent="save" class="metadata-form"><label>版本评分<select v-model.number="rating"><option :value="0">未评分</option><option v-for="score in 5" :key="score" :value="score">{{ score }} / 5</option></select></label><label>版本备注<textarea v-model="notes" rows="2" maxlength="2000" placeholder="例如：弦乐更贴合结尾，但鼓点稍强。" /></label><button class="button small" :disabled="busy">保存评价</button><span v-if="saved" role="status">评价已保存</span><p v-if="error" class="error" role="alert">{{ error }}</p></form></details></template>

import { createApp } from 'vue'
import { createPinia } from 'pinia'
import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router'
import App from './App.vue'
import StudioView from './views/StudioView.vue'
import ProjectsView from './views/ProjectsView.vue'
import LibraryView from './views/LibraryView.vue'
import SettingsView from './views/SettingsView.vue'
import CompareView from './views/CompareView.vue'
import RoadmapView from './views/RoadmapView.vue'
import AboutView from './views/AboutView.vue'
import './styles/main.css'

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(), routes: [
    { path: '/', redirect: '/studio' },
    { path: '/studio', component: StudioView, meta: { title: '创作台' } },
    { path: '/projects', component: ProjectsView, meta: { title: '音乐项目' } },
    { path: '/library', component: LibraryView, meta: { title: '作品库' } },
    { path: '/settings', component: SettingsView, meta: { title: '模型设置' } },
    { path: '/compare', component: CompareView, meta: { title: '版本对比' } },
    { path: '/inspiration', component: RoadmapView, meta: { title: '灵感素材' } },
    { path: '/about', component: AboutView, meta: { title: '关于' } },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/studio' },
  ],
})
createApp(App).use(createPinia()).use(router).mount('#app')

frontend/src/stores/settings.ts （20 行）
import { computed, ref } from 'vue'
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { api } from '../api/client'
import type { ModelSettings } from '../types/settings'
export const useSettings = defineStore('settings', () => {
  // Only public settings enter Pinia. Password inputs are never stored here.
  const data = ref<ModelSettings | null>(null), error = ref(''), connected = ref(false)
  const textIsMock = computed(() => data.value?.text.provider === 'mock')
  const musicIsMock = computed(() => data.value?.music.provider === 'mock')
  const musicLabel = computed(() => data.value?.music.provider === 'elevenlabs' ? 'ElevenLabs Music' : data.value?.music.provider === 'songapi' ? 'SongAPI · Suno' : musicIsMock.value ? 'Mock Music' : 'Google Lyria')
  const modeLabel = computed(() => !data.value ? '正在读取模型状态…' : musicIsMock.value ? (textIsMock.value ? '模拟模式 · 不产生 API 费用' : 'DeepSeek 创作 · 音乐模拟') : `${textIsMock.value ? '设计稿模拟' : 'DeepSeek 创作'} · ${musicLabel.value} 音乐生成`)
  async function load() {
    try { data.value = await api<ModelSettings>('/settings'); connected.value = true; error.value = '' }
    catch (e) { connected.value = false; error.value = (e as Error).message }
  }
  async function save(patch: unknown) {
    data.value = await api<ModelSettings>('/settings', 'PUT', patch); connected.value = true; error.value = ''
  }
  return { data, error, connected, textIsMock, musicIsMock, musicLabel, modeLabel, load, save }
})
frontend/src/stores/studio.ts （120 行）
import { computed, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { api, textRequest } from '../api/client'
import { blueprintErrors } from '../types/validation'
import type { GenerationTask, MusicBlueprint, Project, Purpose } from '../types'

export const useStudio = defineStore('studio', () => {
  const scene = ref('一位旅人走进雨后的森林，发现废弃神殿里还有微弱的光。音乐从安静、好奇，逐渐走向温暖的希望。')
  const purpose = ref<Purpose>('game')
  const blueprint = ref<MusicBlueprint | null>(null)
  const projectId = ref(''), projectName = ref(''), parentVersionId = ref(''), savedDraft = ref('')
  const task = ref<GenerationTask | null>(null)
  const busy = ref(false), error = ref(''), notice = ref('')
  const textProgress = ref(''), revisionInstruction = ref('')
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
  let pollingEpoch = 0
  const active = computed(() => !!task.value && !['completed', 'failed'].includes(task.value.status))
  const draftSnapshot = () => JSON.stringify({ blueprint: blueprint.value, parentVersionId: parentVersionId.value, projectName: projectName.value })
  const dirty = computed(() => !!blueprint.value && draftSnapshot() !== savedDraft.value)
  watch(dirty, value => { (window as Window & { __museflowUnsaved?: boolean }).__museflowUnsaved = value }, { immediate: true })
  function canReplace() { return !busy.value && (!dirty.value || window.confirm('当前设计稿有未保存修改，继续将替换编辑区。确定继续？')) }
  const errors = computed(() => blueprint.value ? blueprintErrors(blueprint.value) : [])
  async function perform(action: () => Promise<void>) {
    busy.value = true; error.value = ''; notice.value = ''
    try { await action() } catch (e) { error.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : '操作失败' }
    finally { busy.value = false; textProgress.value = '' }
  }
  async function generateBlueprint() {
    await perform(async () => {
      const result = await textRequest<{ blueprint: MusicBlueprint; provider: string }>('/blueprints/generate', { userInput: scene.value, purpose: purpose.value }, status => { textProgress.value = status })
      blueprint.value = result.blueprint
      scene.value = result.blueprint.sceneDescription; purpose.value = result.blueprint.purpose
      notice.value = result.provider === 'mock' ? '已创建模拟设计稿：这是固定模板，可自由编辑。' : '创作助手已生成设计稿、情绪时间轴和音乐提示词，并通过 Schema 校验。'
    })
  }
  async function reviseBlueprint() {
    if (!blueprint.value || !revisionInstruction.value.trim() || active.value || busy.value) return
    await perform(async () => {
      const result = await textRequest<{ blueprint: MusicBlueprint; provider: string; message?: string }>('/blueprints/revise', { blueprint: blueprint.value, instruction: revisionInstruction.value }, status => { textProgress.value = status })
      blueprint.value = result.blueprint; scene.value = result.blueprint.sceneDescription; purpose.value = result.blueprint.purpose
      notice.value = result.message ?? '设计稿已根据要求更新，尚未生成新音频。确认参数后可创建新的音乐版本。'
      revisionInstruction.value = ''
    })
  }
  async function persist() {
    if (!blueprint.value) throw new Error('请先生成音乐设计稿')
    if (errors.value.length) throw new Error(errors.value.join('；'))
    const project = await api<Project>(`/projects${projectId.value ? `/${projectId.value}` : ''}`, projectId.value ? 'PUT' : 'POST', { name: projectName.value || blueprint.value.title, blueprint: blueprint.value, draftParentVersionId: parentVersionId.value || null })
    projectId.value = project.id; projectName.value = project.name; blueprint.value = project.blueprint; parentVersionId.value = project.draftParentVersionId ?? ''; savedDraft.value = draftSnapshot()
    return project
  }
  async function save() { await perform(async () => { await persist(); notice.value = '设计稿已保存到本机 SQLite 数据库' }) }
  async function refreshPrompt() {
    await perform(async () => {
      const result = await api<{ blueprint: MusicBlueprint }>('/blueprints/validate', 'POST', { blueprint: blueprint.value })
      blueprint.value = result.blueprint; notice.value = '已校验设计稿；参数有变化时自动刷新提示词。本次校验不调用外部模型。'
    })
  }
  function stopPolling() { pollingEpoch++; clearTimeout(timer) }
  async function poll(id: string, epoch: number) {
    try {
      const result = await api<GenerationTask>(`/generations/${id}`)
      if (epoch !== pollingEpoch) return
      task.value = result; error.value = ''
      if (result.status === 'failed') error.value = result.error
      else if (result.status !== 'completed') timer = setTimeout(() => { void poll(id, epoch) }, 400)
      else notice.value = `${result.provider === 'mock' ? '模拟作品' : result.provider === 'elevenlabs' ? 'ElevenLabs 作品' : result.provider === 'songapi' ? 'SongAPI 作品' : 'Lyria 作品'}已保存。重复生成会创建新版本，历史音频会保留。`
    } catch (e) {
      if (epoch !== pollingEpoch) return
      error.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : '获取任务状态失败'
      timer = setTimeout(() => { void poll(id, epoch) }, 2500)
    }
  }
  async function generateMusic(mode: 'preview' | 'full') {
    if (active.value || busy.value) return
    await perform(async () => {
      const previous = task.value
      task.value = null
      try {
        await persist()
        task.value = await api<GenerationTask>('/generations', 'POST', { projectId: projectId.value, blueprint: blueprint.value, mode, parentVersionId: parentVersionId.value })
        stopPolling(); void poll(task.value.id, pollingEpoch)
      } catch (error) {
        task.value = previous
        throw error
      }
    })
  }
  async function openProject(id: string) {
    if (!canReplace()) return false
    await perform(async () => {
      const project = await api<Project>(`/projects/${id}`)
      stopPolling(); projectId.value = project.id; projectName.value = project.name; blueprint.value = project.blueprint; parentVersionId.value = project.draftParentVersionId ?? ''; savedDraft.value = draftSnapshot()
      scene.value = project.sceneDescription; purpose.value = project.blueprint.purpose
      task.value = project.tasks?.find(t => !['completed', 'failed'].includes(t.status)) ?? project.tasks?.find(t => !t.audioDeleted) ?? null
      if (active.value && task.value) void poll(task.value.id, pollingEpoch)
    })
    return !error.value
  }
  async function branch(versionId: string) {
    if (active.value || !canReplace()) return false
    await perform(async () => {
      const result = await api<{ project: Project }>(`/versions/${versionId}/branch`, 'POST', {})
      const project = result.project
      stopPolling(); projectId.value = project.id; projectName.value = project.name; blueprint.value = project.blueprint
      scene.value = project.sceneDescription; purpose.value = project.blueprint.purpose
      parentVersionId.value = versionId; task.value = null; savedDraft.value = draftSnapshot()
      notice.value = '已从所选版本创建分支草稿。修改设计稿后生成，会保留父版本与旧音频。'
    })
    return !error.value
  }
  function forgetVersion(id: string, generationId: string) {
    if (parentVersionId.value === id) parentVersionId.value = ''
    if (task.value?.id === generationId) task.value = null
  }
  function reset() {
    stopPolling(); blueprint.value = null; projectId.value = ''; projectName.value = ''; parentVersionId.value = ''; savedDraft.value = ''; task.value = null; error.value = ''; notice.value = ''
  }
  return { scene, purpose, blueprint, projectId, projectName, parentVersionId, dirty, branch, forgetVersion, task, busy, error, notice, active, errors, textProgress, revisionInstruction, generateBlueprint, reviseBlueprint, save, refreshPrompt, generateMusic, openProject, reset }
})

export type Purpose = 'game' | 'video' | 'animation' | 'story' | 'song' | 'other'
export interface MusicSection {
  id: string; name: string; startSeconds: number; endSeconds: number
  emotion: string; intensity: number; instruments: string[]; description: string
}
export interface MusicBlueprint {
  id: string; title: string; sceneDescription: string; purpose: Purpose
  genre: string; subGenre?: string; mood: string[]; bpm?: number; key?: string
  instruments: string[]; vocalMode: 'instrumental' | 'vocal'; vocalGender?: string
  language?: string; lyrics?: string; targetDurationSeconds: number; strictDuration: boolean
  structure: MusicSection[]; negativePrompt?: string; finalLyriaPrompt: string
  promptSource?: 'local' | 'deepseek'; promptFingerprint?: string
}
export type TaskState = 'queued' | 'preparing' | 'generating' | 'post_processing' | 'completed' | 'failed'
export interface GenerationTask {
  id: string; projectId: string; blueprint: MusicBlueprint; mode: 'preview' | 'full'
  status: TaskState; progress: number; error: string; provider: 'mock' | 'lyria' | 'songapi' | 'elevenlabs'
  format?: 'wav' | 'mp3'; strictDurationApplied?: boolean; sourceDurationSeconds?: number; songText?: string[]; songStructure?: unknown[];
  actualDurationSeconds?: number; modelTargetDurationSeconds: number
  parentVersionId?: string | null; audioDeleted?: boolean; versionId?: string
  createdAt: string; updatedAt: string
}
export interface Project {
  id: string; name: string; sceneDescription: string; blueprint: MusicBlueprint
  createdAt: string; updatedAt: string; tasks?: GenerationTask[]; draftParentVersionId?: string | null
}
export interface Version {

  title: string; blueprint: MusicBlueprint; mode: 'preview' | 'full'; provider: 'mock' | 'lyria' | 'songapi' | 'elevenlabs'
  model?: string; format: 'wav' | 'mp3'; generationPrompt?: string; songText?: string[]; songStructure?: unknown[]; sourceDurationSeconds?: number; actualDurationSeconds: number; modelTargetDurationSeconds: number
  strictDurationApplied: boolean; rating: number; notes: string; createdAt: string
}

  post_processing: '检测、处理并保存音频', completed: '作品已保存', failed: '生成失败',

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { blueprintErrors, splitTags } from './validation'
import type { MusicBlueprint } from './index'
const sample = (): MusicBlueprint => ({
  id: 'blueprint', title: '森林', sceneDescription: '旅人走进森林', purpose: 'game', genre: '氛围',
  mood: ['静谧'], bpm: 88, instruments: ['钢琴'], vocalMode: 'instrumental',
  targetDurationSeconds: 60, strictDuration: false, finalLyriaPrompt: '',
  structure: [
    { id: 'one', name: '序章', startSeconds: 0, endSeconds: 20, emotion: '平静', intensity: 3, instruments: [], description: '' },
    { id: 'two', name: '展开', startSeconds: 20, endSeconds: 60, emotion: '希望', intensity: 6, instruments: [], description: '' },
  ],
})
describe('音乐设计稿表单校验', () => {
  it('接受相邻、不重叠的段落', () => expect(blueprintErrors(sample())).toEqual([]))
  it('允许时间轴按任意编辑顺序排列', () => { const b = sample(); b.structure.reverse(); expect(blueprintErrors(b)).toEqual([]) })
  it('检测重叠', () => { const b = sample(); b.structure[1]!.startSeconds = 19; expect(blueprintErrors(b).join()).toContain('重叠') })
  it('检测错误起止时间', () => { const b = sample(); b.structure[0]!.endSeconds = 0; expect(blueprintErrors(b).join()).toContain('开始时间') })
  it('检测超出目标时长', () => { const b = sample(); b.targetDurationSeconds = 30; expect(blueprintErrors(b).join()).toContain('超出目标') })
  it('检测小数强度和错误 BPM', () => { const b = sample(); b.bpm = 400; b.structure[0]!.intensity = 2.5; expect(blueprintErrors(b)).toHaveLength(2) })
  it('检测空时间轴', () => { const b = sample(); b.structure = []; expect(blueprintErrors(b).join()).toContain('1～32') })
  it('拆分中英文乐器分隔符', () => expect(splitTags('钢琴、 弦乐，鼓, 合成器\n')).toEqual(['钢琴', '弦乐', '鼓', '合成器']))
})
frontend/src/types/validation.ts （22 行）
import type { MusicBlueprint } from './index'
export const splitTags = (value: string): string[] => value.split(/[,，、\n]/).map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean)
export function blueprintErrors(b: MusicBlueprint): string[] {
  const errors: string[] = []
  if (!b.title.trim()) errors.push('请填写作品标题')
  if (!b.sceneDescription.trim()) errors.push('请填写场景描述')
  if (!b.genre.trim()) errors.push('请填写曲风')
  if (!Number.isInteger(b.bpm) || b.bpm! < 40 || b.bpm! > 240) errors.push('BPM 必须是 40～240 之间的整数')
  if (!Number.isFinite(b.targetDurationSeconds) || b.targetDurationSeconds < 5 || b.targetDurationSeconds > 600) errors.push('目标时长应为 5～600 秒')
  if (!b.structure.length || b.structure.length > 32) errors.push('时间轴应包含 1～32 个段落')
  const sorted = [...b.structure].sort((a, c) => a.startSeconds - c.startSeconds)
  let lastEnd = 0
  for (const s of sorted) {
    if (!s.name.trim()) errors.push('请填写段落名称')
    if (!Number.isFinite(s.startSeconds) || !Number.isFinite(s.endSeconds) || s.startSeconds < 0 || s.startSeconds >= s.endSeconds) errors.push(`${s.name}：开始时间必须小于结束时间，且不能为负数`)
    if (s.startSeconds < lastEnd) errors.push(`${s.name}：时间段重叠`)
    if (s.endSeconds > b.targetDurationSeconds) errors.push(`${s.name}：超出目标时长`)
    if (!Number.isInteger(s.intensity) || s.intensity < 1 || s.intensity > 10) errors.push(`${s.name}：强度应为 1～10 的整数`)
    lastEnd = s.endSeconds
  }
  return [...new Set(errors)]
}
frontend/src/types/versions.test.ts （11 行）
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { versionDifferences, versionTree, switchPosition } from './versions'
import type { Version } from './index'
const version = (id: string, parentVersionId: string | null = null): Version => ({ id, parentVersionId, projectId: 'project', generationId: id, title: '测试', mode: 'full', provider: 'mock', format: 'wav', actualDurationSeconds: 8, modelTargetDurationSeconds: 60, strictDurationApplied: false, rating: 0, notes: '', createdAt: '', blueprint: { id, title: '测试', sceneDescription: '森林', purpose: 'game', genre: 'Ambient', bpm: 88, mood: [], instruments: [], vocalMode: 'instrumental', targetDurationSeconds: 60, strictDuration: false, finalLyriaPrompt: 'forest', structure: [{ id, name: 'intro', startSeconds: 0, endSeconds: 60, intensity: 5, emotion: 'calm', instruments: [], description: '' }] } })
describe('version comparison', () => {
  it('ignores random IDs and metadata while comparing musical content', () => { const a = version('a'), b = version('b'); b.rating = 5; b.notes = 'great'; expect(versionDifferences(a, b)).toEqual([]) })
  it('compares actual generation prompt, BPM and timeline', () => { const a = version('a'), b = version('b'); b.blueprint.bpm = 112; b.blueprint.structure[0]!.intensity = 9; b.generationPrompt = 'preview'; expect(versionDifferences(a, b).map(x => x.label)).toEqual(['BPM', '情绪时间轴', '实际发送的提示词']) })
  it('orders a branched tree without hiding a missing-parent record', () => { expect(versionTree([version('orphan', 'missing'), version('sibling', 'root'), version('grand', 'child'), version('child', 'root'), version('root')]).map(x => [x.version.id, x.depth])).toEqual([['root', 0], ['child', 1], ['grand', 2], ['sibling', 1], ['orphan', 0]]) })
  it('does not loop or hide records with damaged cyclic lineage', () => { expect(versionTree([version('a', 'b'), version('b', 'a')])).toHaveLength(2) })
  it('keeps absolute playback time and clamps to a shorter track', () => { expect(switchPosition(2, 8)).toBe(2); expect(switchPosition(10, 5)).toBe(4.95); expect(switchPosition(NaN, 5)).toBe(0); expect(switchPosition(1, 0)).toBe(0) })
})
frontend/src/types/versions.ts （36 行）
import type { Version } from './index'
export interface Difference { label: string; a: string; b: string }
const labels: Record<string, string> = { title: '作品标题', sceneDescription: '场景描述', purpose: '用途', genre: '曲风', subGenre: '子曲风', mood: '情绪', bpm: 'BPM', key: '调性', instruments: '乐器', vocalMode: '人声模式', vocalGender: '人声音色', language: '语言', lyrics: '歌词', targetDurationSeconds: '目标时长', strictDuration: '严格时长', negativePrompt: '避免元素' }
const display = (value: unknown) => value === undefined || value === '' ? '—' : typeof value === 'string' ? value : JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)
export function versionDifferences(a: Version, b: Version): Difference[] {
  const rows: Difference[] = []
  const add = (label: string, left: unknown, right: unknown) => { if (JSON.stringify(left) !== JSON.stringify(right)) rows.push({ label, a: display(left), b: display(right) }) }
  for (const [key, label] of Object.entries(labels)) add(label, a.blueprint[key as keyof typeof a.blueprint], b.blueprint[key as keyof typeof b.blueprint])
  const structure = (v: Version) => v.blueprint.structure.map(({ id: _id, ...section }) => section)
  add('情绪时间轴', structure(a), structure(b))
  add('生成模式', a.mode, b.mode); add('音乐服务商', a.provider, b.provider); add('音乐模型', a.model, b.model); add('输出格式', a.format, b.format)
  add('模型目标时长', a.modelTargetDurationSeconds, b.modelTargetDurationSeconds)
  add('实际发送的提示词', a.generationPrompt ?? a.blueprint.finalLyriaPrompt, b.generationPrompt ?? b.blueprint.finalLyriaPrompt)
  return rows
}
export function versionTree(versions: Version[]): { version: Version; depth: number }[] {
  const ids = new Set(versions.map(v => v.id)), children = new Map<string, Version[]>(), roots: Version[] = []
  for (const version of [...versions].reverse()) {
    if (version.parentVersionId && ids.has(version.parentVersionId)) children.set(version.parentVersionId, [...(children.get(version.parentVersionId) ?? []), version])
    else roots.push(version)
  }
  const result: { version: Version; depth: number }[] = [], seen = new Set<string>(), stack = roots.reverse().map(version => ({ version, depth: 0 }))
  while (stack.length) {
    const item = stack.pop()!
    if (seen.has(item.version.id)) continue
    seen.add(item.version.id); result.push(item)
    for (const version of [...(children.get(item.version.id) ?? [])].reverse()) stack.push({ version, depth: item.depth + 1 })
  }
  // Legacy or damaged lineage must not hide records.
  for (const version of versions) if (!seen.has(version.id)) result.push({ version, depth: 0 })
  return result
}
export function switchPosition(current: number, nextDuration: number) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(current) || current < 0 || !Number.isFinite(nextDuration) || nextDuration <= 0) return 0
  return Math.min(current, Math.max(0, nextDuration - .05))
}

<template><div class="page-heading"><div><span class="eyebrow">ABOUT MUSEFLOW</span><h1>为故事创作者而作</h1><p>MuseFlow · 本地优先的 AI 场景配乐工作台</p></div><span class="subtle-tag">v0.4.0 / 桌面版</span></div><section class="card about-card"><h2>先设计音乐，再生成声音</h2><p>把场景与情绪整理成可编辑的音乐设计稿，通过时间轴安排音乐的起承转合。每次生成保留独立音频和参数快照，帮助你持续探索。</p><h3>当前可用</h3><p>DeepSeek / Mock 设计稿、自然语言改稿、歌词与时间轴编辑、独立模型设置、Lyria / Mock 后台音乐生成、FFmpeg 严格时长、项目管理、版本分支树、A/B 播放、差异对比、评分备注、MP3 / WAV 下载和设计记录导出。</p><h3>后续阶段</h3><p>灵感素材管理、Linux Secret Service 与 AppImage 尚未实现。真实模型账户调用需要在设置中配置 Key 后验证。</p><h3>本机数据</h3><p>遵守 XDG 目录规范：配置位于 ~/.config/museflow，数据库与音频位于 ~/.local/share/museflow，缓存位于 ~/.cache/museflow。可通过 XDG 环境变量调整。</p><h3>关于模拟音频</h3><p>测试音频由项目内置脚本合成，长度 8 秒，用于验证播放与存储。曲风和歌词不会改变原始模拟音频；严格时长会裁剪或补静音。默认 Mock 不调用外部模型，选择真实服务后会产生对应 API 请求。</p></section></template>

import { computed, onMounted, ref, watch } from 'vue'

import type { Project, Version } from '../types'
import { versionDifferences, versionTree } from '../types/versions'
import ComparePlayer from '../components/ComparePlayer.vue'
import VersionMetadata from '../components/VersionMetadata.vue'
import { useStudio } from '../stores/studio'
const route = useRoute(), router = useRouter(), studio = useStudio()
const projects = ref<Project[]>([]), versions = ref<Version[]>([]), projectId = ref(typeof route.query.project === 'string' ? route.query.project : '')
const aId = ref(typeof route.query.a === 'string' ? route.query.a : ''), bId = ref(''), error = ref(''), loading = ref(true)
const filtered = computed(() => versions.value.filter(v => !projectId.value || v.projectId === projectId.value))
const tree = computed(() => versionTree(filtered.value))
const a = computed(() => filtered.value.find(v => v.id === aId.value)), b = computed(() => filtered.value.find(v => v.id === bId.value))
const differences = computed(() => a.value && b.value ? versionDifferences(a.value, b.value) : [])
watch(filtered, values => { if (!values.some(v => v.id === aId.value)) aId.value = values[0]?.id ?? ''; if (!values.some(v => v.id === bId.value) || bId.value === aId.value) bId.value = values.find(v => v.id !== aId.value)?.id ?? '' })
async function load() { try { [projects.value, versions.value] = await Promise.all([api<Project[]>('/projects'), api<Version[]>('/versions')]); error.value = '' } catch (e) { error.value = (e as Error).message } finally { loading.value = false } }
async function branch(id: string) { if (await studio.branch(id)) await router.push('/studio'); else if (studio.error) error.value = studio.error }
onMounted(load)
watch(() => studio.task?.status, state => { if (state === 'completed') void load() })

'模拟' : 'Lyria' }}{{ node.version.parentVersionId ? ' · 父版本 ' + node.version.parentVersionId.slice(0, 8) : ' · 独立版本' }}</small></div></div><div class="version-actions"><button class="button small" @click="aId = node.version.id">设为 A</button><button class="button small" @click="bId = node.version.id">设为 B</button><button class="text-button" @click="branch(node.version.id)" :disabled="studio.busy || studio.active">创建分支</button></div></li></ul></section><section class="card compare-select"><div class="form-grid two"><label>版本 A<select v-model="aId"><option v-for="v in filtered" :key="v.id" :value="v.id">{{ v.title }} · {{ v.blueprint.bpm }} BPM · {{ v.id.slice(0, 8) }}</option></select></label><label>版本 B<select v-model="bId"><option value="">请选择第二个版本</option><option v-for="v in filtered" :key="v.id" :value="v.id">{{ v.title }} · {{ v.blueprint.bpm }} BPM · {{ v.id.slice(0, 8) }}</option></select></label></div><p v-if="aId && aId === bId" class="muted">当前两侧选择了同一版本，请选择另一个版本进行比较。</p></section><template v-if="a && b && a.id !== b.id"><ComparePlayer :a="a" :b="b" /><section class="card"><h2>参数与提示词差异 <span class="subtle-tag">{{ differences.length }} 项</span></h2><p v-if="!differences.length" class="muted">生成参数相同；模型仍可能生成不同音乐。</p><div v-else class="diff-table-wrap"><table class="diff-table"><thead><tr><th>字段</th><th>版本 A</th><th>版本 B</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr v-for="difference in differences" :key="difference.label"><th>{{ difference.label }}</th><td><pre>{{ difference.a }}</pre></td><td><pre>{{ difference.b }}</pre></td></tr></tbody></table></div></section><div class="ab-metadata"><section class="card"><h2>A · {{ a.title }}</h2><VersionMetadata :version="a" @saved="load" /></section><section class="card"><h2>B · {{ b.title }}</h2><VersionMetadata :version="b" @saved="load" /></section></div></template></template></template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onMounted, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import VersionMetadata from '../components/VersionMetadata.vue'
import { api } from '../api/client'
import type { Version, Project } from '../types'
import AudioPlayer from '../components/AudioPlayer.vue'
import { useStudio } from '../stores/studio'
const studio = useStudio(), router = useRouter()
async function branch(id: string) { if (await studio.branch(id)) await router.push('/studio'); else if (studio.error) error.value = studio.error }
async function remove(version: Version) {
  if (!window.confirm(`删除“${version.title}”的这个版本及原始音频？生成任务记录会保留，此操作无法恢复。`)) return
  try { const result = await api<{ audioCleanupPending: boolean }>(`/versions/${version.id}`, 'DELETE'); studio.forgetVersion(version.id, version.generationId); await load(); if (result.audioCleanupPending) error.value = '版本已删除，部分文件清理失败，请检查数据目录权限' } catch (e) { error.value = (e as Error).message }
}
const versions = ref<Version[]>([]), projects = ref<Project[]>([]), query = ref(''), projectId = ref(''), error = ref(''), loading = ref(true)
const filtered = computed(() => versions.value.filter(v => (!projectId.value || v.projectId === projectId.value) && `${v.title} ${v.blueprint.genre} ${v.blueprint.sceneDescription}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.value.toLowerCase())))
async function load() {
  try { [versions.value, projects.value] = await Promise.all([api<Version[]>('/versions'), api<Project[]>('/projects')]); error.value = '' }
  catch (e) { error.value = (e as Error).message }
  finally { loading.value = false }
}
onMounted(load)
watch(() => studio.task?.status, status => { if (status === 'completed') void load() })

frontend/src/views/ProjectsView.vue （39 行）
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { api } from '../api/client'
import { useStudio } from '../stores/studio'
import type { Project } from '../types'
import { purposes, taskLabels } from '../types'
const query = ref(''), history = ref<Project | null>(null), editing = ref(''), newName = ref('')
const projects = ref<Project[]>([]), error = ref(''), loading = ref(true)
const router = useRouter(), studio = useStudio()
const filtered = computed(() => projects.value.filter(p => `${p.name} ${p.sceneDescription}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.value.toLowerCase())))
async function rename(project: Project) {
  try { const saved = await api<Project>(`/projects/${project.id}`, 'PUT', { name: newName.value, blueprint: project.blueprint }); if (studio.projectId === project.id) studio.projectName = saved.name; editing.value = ''; await load() } catch (e) { error.value = (e as Error).message }
}
async function showHistory(id: string) { try { history.value = await api<Project>(`/projects/${id}`) } catch (e) { error.value = (e as Error).message } }
async function load() {
  loading.value = true
  try { projects.value = await api<Project[]>('/projects') } catch (e) { error.value = (e as Error).message }
  finally { loading.value = false }
}
async function open(id: string) { const opened = await studio.openProject(id); if (opened) await router.push('/studio'); else error.value = studio.error }
async function remove(project: Project) {
  if (!window.confirm(`删除“${project.name}”？这会同时删除该项目的设计稿、任务和全部音频，无法恢复。`)) return
  try { const result = await api<{ audioCleanupPending: boolean }>(`/projects/${project.id}`, 'DELETE'); if (studio.projectId === project.id) studio.reset(); await load(); error.value = result.audioCleanupPending ? '数据库记录已删除，但部分音频清理失败，请检查目录权限' : '' }
  catch (e) { error.value = (e as Error).message }
}
onMounted(load)
</script>
<template>

' · 音频已删除' : '' }}</strong><span>{{ task.mode === 'preview' ? '试听' : '完整歌曲' }} · {{ task.provider }} · {{ new Date(task.createdAt).toLocaleString('zh-CN') }}</span><p v-if="task.error" class="error">{{ task.error }}</p><small>任务 {{ task.id.slice(0, 8) }}{{ task.parentVersionId ? ' · 父版本 ' + task.parentVersionId.slice(0, 8) : '' }}</small></li></ul><button class="button small" @click="showHistory(history.id)">刷新记录</button></section>
  <p v-if="error" class="error message" role="alert">{{ error }}</p>
  <p v-if="loading" class="muted">正在读取本地项目…</p>

    <article v-for="project in filtered" :key="project.id" class="card project-card"><div class="project-top"><span class="project-icon">♫</span><span class="subtle-tag">{{ purposes[project.blueprint.purpose] }}</span></div><form v-if="editing === project.id" @submit.prevent="rename(project)"><label>项目新名称<input v-model="newName" maxlength="100" /></label><button class="button small" type="submit">保存名称</button><button class="text-button" type="button" @click="editing = ''">取消</button></form><h2 v-else>{{ project.name }}</h2><p class="project-description">{{ project.sceneDescription }}</p><div class="tag-row"><span>{{ project.blueprint.genre }}</span><span>{{ project.blueprint.bpm }} BPM</span><span>{{ project.blueprint.targetDurationSeconds }} 秒目标</span></div><small class="muted">更新于 {{ new Date(project.updatedAt).toLocaleString('zh-CN') }}</small><div class="project-actions"><button class="button small" @click="open(project.id)" :disabled="studio.busy">打开设计稿 →</button><button class="text-button danger" @click="remove(project)">删除项目</button></div><div class="version-actions"><button class="text-button" @click="editing = project.id; newName = project.name">重命名</button><button class="text-button" @click="showHistory(project.id)">生成记录</button><RouterLink class="text-link" :to="{ path: '/compare', query: { project: project.id } }">版本树与对比</RouterLink><a class="text-link" :href="`/api/projects/${project.id}/export`" download>导出设计与记录</a></div></article>

frontend/src/views/SettingsView.vue （123 行）
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onBeforeUnmount, onMounted, reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { textRequest } from '../api/client'
import { useSettings } from '../stores/settings'
import { useStudio } from '../stores/studio'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { api } from '../api/client'
import type { MusicBlueprint, Project } from '../types'
const studio = useStudio(), router = useRouter()
const settings = useSettings()
const textForm = reactive({ provider: 'mock', baseUrl: 'https://api.deepseek.com', model: 'deepseek-v4-flash', connectTimeoutSeconds: 10, requestTimeoutSeconds: 90 })
const musicForm = reactive({ provider: 'mock', model: 'lyria-3-clip-preview', songapiModel: 'v5', outputFormat: 'wav', previewFirst: true })
const textKey = ref(''), musicKey = ref(''), error = ref(''), notice = ref(''), progress = ref(''), busy = ref(false)
const ready = ref(false)
const isElevenLabs = computed(() => musicForm.provider === 'elevenlabs')
const isSongApi = computed(() => musicForm.provider === 'songapi')
const musicKeyLabel = computed(() => isElevenLabs.value ? 'ElevenLabs API Key' : isSongApi.value ? 'SongAPI Key' : 'Google Gemini API Key')
const musicEnvironment = computed(() => isElevenLabs.value ? 'ELEVENLABS_API_KEY' : isSongApi.value ? 'SONGAPI_KEY' : 'GEMINI_API_KEY')
const musicKeySource = computed(() => isElevenLabs.value ? settings.data?.music.elevenlabsKeySource : isSongApi.value ? settings.data?.music.songapiKeySource : settings.data?.music.lyriaKeySource)
function sync() {
  const current = settings.data
  if (!current) return
  Object.keys(textForm).forEach(key => { (textForm as Record<string, unknown>)[key] = current.text[key as keyof typeof current.text] })
  Object.keys(musicForm).forEach(key => { (musicForm as Record<string, unknown>)[key] = current.music[key as keyof typeof current.music] })
}
onMounted(async () => { await settings.load(); sync(); ready.value = true })
onBeforeUnmount(() => { textKey.value = ''; musicKey.value = '' })
async function perform(action: () => Promise<void>) {
  busy.value = true; error.value = ''; notice.value = ''; progress.value = ''
  try { await action() } catch (e) { error.value = (e as Error).message }
  finally { busy.value = false; progress.value = ''; textKey.value = ''; musicKey.value = '' }
}
async function saveText(test = false) {
  await perform(async () => {
    await settings.save({ text: { ...textForm, ...(textKey.value ? { apiKey: textKey.value } : {}) } })
    textKey.value = ''
    if (test) {
      try {
        const result = await textRequest<{ ok: boolean; message: string }>('/providers/text/test', {}, value => { progress.value = value })
        notice.value = result.message
      } finally { await settings.load() }
    } else notice.value = '创作助手设置已保存。界面输入的 Key 仅保存在后端进程内存。'
  })
}
async function saveMusic() {
  await perform(async () => {
    await settings.save({ music: { ...musicForm, ...(musicKey.value ? { apiKey: musicKey.value } : {}) } })
    notice.value = '音乐模型设置已保存。Key 仅保存在本次后端会话，重启软件后需要重新填写；点击“保存并测试密钥”可检查连接。'
  })
}
async function keyAction(kind: 'text' | 'music', action: 'clearApiKey' | 'useEnvironmentKey') {
  await perform(async () => { await settings.save({ [kind]: { ...(kind === 'music' ? { ...musicForm } : {}), [action]: true } }); notice.value = action === 'clearApiKey' ? '已清除会话 Key，并在本次会话停用对应环境变量 Key。' : '已恢复使用对应环境变量 Key（若已设置）。' })
}
async function testMusic() {
  await perform(async () => {
    await settings.save({ music: { ...musicForm, ...(musicKey.value ? { apiKey: musicKey.value } : {}) } }); musicKey.value = ''
    try { const result = await textRequest<{ message: string }>('/providers/music/test', {}, value => { progress.value = value }); notice.value = result.message }
    finally { await settings.load() }
  })
}
async function generateTestMusic() {
  await perform(async () => {
    if (studio.busy || studio.active) throw new Error('请等待当前创作任务完成后再生成测试音乐')
    await settings.save({ music: { ...musicForm, ...(musicKey.value ? { apiKey: musicKey.value } : {}) } }); musicKey.value = ''
    // A local blueprint fixture avoids an unintended paid text-model request.
    const blueprint: MusicBlueprint = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), title: '音乐服务测试', sceneDescription: '温暖、轻柔的原声吉他纯音乐', purpose: 'other', genre: 'Acoustic', mood: ['温暖'], bpm: 90, key: 'C major', instruments: ['吉他'], vocalMode: 'instrumental', targetDurationSeconds: 30, strictDuration: false, structure: [{ id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: '试听', startSeconds: 0, endSeconds: 30, emotion: '温暖', intensity: 4, instruments: ['吉他'], description: '轻柔的旋律，自然结束' }], finalLyriaPrompt: '' }
    const project = await api<Project>('/projects', 'POST', { name: blueprint.title, blueprint })
    if (!await studio.openProject(project.id)) return; await studio.generateMusic('preview'); await router.push('/studio')
  })
}
const keySource = (source?: string) => source === 'session' ? '后端会话内存' : source === 'environment' ? '后端环境变量' : '尚未配置'
</script>
<template>
  <div class="page-heading"><div><span class="eyebrow">MODEL CONNECTIONS</span><h1>模型设置</h1><p>创作助手与音乐生成服务独立配置，所有模型请求由本地后端发出。</p></div></div>
  <p v-if="error || settings.error" class="error message" role="alert">{{ error || settings.error }}</p>
  <p v-if="notice" class="success message" role="status">{{ notice }}</p>
  <p v-if="progress" class="mock-notice" role="status">{{ progress }} 可以继续访问其他页面，音乐任务不受影响。</p>
  <div class="settings-grid">
    <section class="card">
      <span class="eyebrow">TEXT PROVIDER</span><h2>创作助手模型</h2><p class="muted">理解场景、编写歌词、设计情绪时间轴，并生成音乐提示词。</p>
      <fieldset :disabled="busy || !ready || !settings.data" class="settings-fields">
        <label>创作助手服务商<select v-model="textForm.provider" aria-label="创作助手服务商"><option value="mock">Mock · 免费模拟</option><option value="deepseek">DeepSeek / Chat Completions 兼容服务</option></select></label>
        <label>API 地址<input v-model="textForm.baseUrl" aria-label="API 地址" type="url" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" maxlength="2048" placeholder="https://api.deepseek.com" /><small>支持基础地址、/v1 地址或完整 /chat/completions 地址。</small></label>
        <label>模型名称<input v-model="textForm.model" aria-label="模型名称" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" maxlength="160" list="text-models" /><datalist id="text-models"><option value="deepseek-v4-flash" /><option value="deepseek-v4-pro" /></datalist></label>
        <label>DeepSeek API Key<input v-model="textKey" aria-label="DeepSeek API Key" type="password" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" maxlength="8192" placeholder="留空保留当前 Key；保存后清空输入框" /><small>当前来源：{{ keySource(settings.data?.text.keySource) }}。Key 不回传浏览器、不写入磁盘。</small></label>
        <div class="key-actions"><button class="text-button" @click="keyAction('text', 'clearApiKey')">清除创作助手 Key</button><button class="text-button" @click="keyAction('text', 'useEnvironmentKey')">使用 DEEPSEEK_API_KEY</button></div>
        <details><summary>网络超时</summary><div class="form-grid two"><label>连接超时（秒）<input v-model.number="textForm.connectTimeoutSeconds" type="number" min="1" max="30" /></label><label>响应超时（秒）<input v-model.number="textForm.requestTimeoutSeconds" type="number" min="2" max="180" /></label></div></details>

      <p v-else class="small-note">测试密钥仅查询所选模型信息，不生成音乐。生成测试音乐会创建独立项目，并使用 30 秒试听模型；真实模式按服务商计费。</p>
    </section>
  </div>
</template>

frontend/tests/desktop.mjs （43 行）
import { chromium, expect } from '@playwright/test'
const browser = await chromium.connectOverCDP('http://127.0.0.1:18083', { timeout: 20000, noDefaults: true })
try {
  const context = browser.contexts()[0]
  let page
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 100; attempt++) {
    page = context.pages().find(p => p.url().startsWith('http://127.0.0.1:18080/'))
    if (page) break
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100))
  }
  if (!page) throw new Error('Qt desktop did not load the local interface')
  const agent = await page.evaluate(() => navigator.userAgent)
  if (!agent.includes('QtWebEngine')) throw new Error('Test did not connect to Qt WebEngine')
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: '从一个场景，开始一段音乐' })).toBeVisible()
  await page.evaluate(async () => { await fetch('/api/settings', { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ music: { provider: 'mock', outputFormat: 'mp3' } }) }) })
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'AI 生成音乐设计稿' }).click()
  await page.getByLabel('作品标题').fill('桌面窗口测试')
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '生成 30 秒试听 · 模拟' }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText('作品已保存', { exact: true })).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 })
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: '↓ 下载 MP3' })).toBeVisible()
  const audio = page.locator('audio')
  await audio.evaluate(async player => { await player.play() })
  await expect.poll(() => audio.evaluate(player => player.currentTime)).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  await audio.evaluate(player => player.pause())
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: '作品库', exact: true }).click()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '从此版本创建分支' }).click()
  await page.getByLabel('BPM', { exact: true }).fill('120')
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '生成完整歌曲 · 模拟' }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText('作品已保存', { exact: true })).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 })
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: '版本对比', exact: true }).click()
  await expect(page.locator('.version-tree li')).toHaveCount(2)
  await expect(page.locator('.diff-table')).toContainText('120')
  await page.getByLabel('循环播放当前版本').check()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '播放当前版本', exact: true }).click()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '切换到 B' }).click()
  await expect.poll(() => page.getByLabel('对比 B 音频').evaluate(player => !player.paused)).toBe(true)
  await expect.poll(() => page.getByLabel('对比 A 音频').evaluate(player => player.paused)).toBe(true)
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '暂停', exact: true }).click()
  await page.screenshot({ path: 'test-results/desktop-qt-compare.png', fullPage: true })
  const blocked = await page.evaluate(async () => { try { await fetch('https://example.com/'); return false } catch { return true } })
  if (!blocked) throw new Error('Desktop allowed an external renderer request')
  console.log('PASS: real Qt WebEngine window, create/generate/play, branching/A-B, blocked external requests')
} finally { await browser.close() }

scripts/build.sh （9 行）
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
museflow_root="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cmake -S "$museflow_root/backend" -B "$museflow_root/build" -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DMUSEFLOW_BUILD_DESKTOP=ON
cmake --build "$museflow_root/build" --parallel "${MUSEFLOW_BUILD_JOBS:-2}"
cd "$museflow_root/frontend"
npm ci --no-audit --no-fund
npm run build
echo '构建完成。运行 scripts/run.sh 启动桌面应用。'
scripts/desktop_test.py （57 行）
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Exercise the real desktop executable in an offscreen display, with isolated user directories."""
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from urllib import request
from smoke_test import ROOT, call, stop, wait_ready

def main():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='museflow-desktop-test-') as directory:
        runtime = Path(directory) / 'runtime'; runtime.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        alsa = Path(directory) / 'alsa.conf'; alsa.write_text('pcm.!default { type null }\n')
        env = dict(os.environ, XDG_DATA_HOME=directory + '/data', XDG_CONFIG_HOME=directory + '/config', XDG_CACHE_HOME=directory + '/cache', XDG_RUNTIME_DIR=str(runtime), QT_QPA_PLATFORM='offscreen', QTWEBENGINE_REMOTE_DEBUGGING='127.0.0.1:18083', QTWEBENGINE_CHROMIUM_FLAGS='--disable-gpu', ALSA_CONFIG_PATH=str(alsa), NO_PROXY='127.0.0.1,localhost', no_proxy='127.0.0.1,localhost')
        env['DEEPSEEK_API_KEY'] = 'fixture-desktop-environment'
        env['ELEVENLABS_API_KEY'] = 'fixture-elevenlabs-desktop-environment'
        env.pop('GEMINI_API_KEY', None); env.pop('MUSEFLOW_GOOGLE_TEST_BASE', None)
        for port in (18080, 18083):
            with socket.socket() as probe:
                probe.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1); probe.bind(('127.0.0.1', port))
        log = Path(directory) / 'desktop.log'
        with log.open('wb') as output:
            process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'scripts/run.sh')], env=env, stdout=output, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            try:
                wait_ready(process)
                assert call('/api/health')['desktopSession'], 'Desktop must own its backend'
                assert call('/api/settings')['text']['keySource'] == 'environment', 'Backend lost environment key'
                assert call('/api/settings')['music']['elevenlabsKeySource'] == 'environment', 'Backend lost ElevenLabs environment key'
                opener = request.build_opener(request.ProxyHandler({}))
                for _ in range(100):
                    if process.poll() is not None: raise RuntimeError('Desktop exited early')
                    try:
                        with opener.open('http://127.0.0.1:18083/json/version', timeout=1): break
                    except OSError: time.sleep(.1)
                else: raise RuntimeError('Qt debug endpoint unavailable')
                duplicate = subprocess.run([str(ROOT / 'scripts/run.sh')], env=env, stdout=output, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=8)
                assert duplicate.returncode == 0 and process.poll() is None, 'Single instance activation failed'
                (ROOT / 'frontend/test-results').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
                subprocess.run(['node', 'tests/desktop.mjs'], cwd=ROOT / 'frontend', env=env, check=True, timeout=90)
                assert process.poll() is None, 'Disconnecting developer tools must keep the app open'
                stop(process)
                for _ in range(50):
                    try: call('/api/health')
                    except OSError: break
                    time.sleep(.1)
                else: raise AssertionError('Desktop left an orphan backend')
                assert b'fixture-desktop-environment' not in log.read_bytes(), 'Key leaked to desktop log'
                # Restart keeps versions while clearing renderer profile state.
                process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'scripts/run.sh')], env=env, stdout=output, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
                wait_ready(process); assert len(call('/api/versions')) == 2
                print('PASS: desktop single instance, environment key handoff, backend ownership, shutdown cleanup and restart persistence')
            except Exception:
                print(log.read_text()[-4000:]); raise
            finally: stop(process)
if __name__ == '__main__': main()
scripts/dev.sh （42 行）
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
museflow_root="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
for dependency in cmake c++ npm python3 curl setsid; do
  command -v "$dependency" >/dev/null || { echo "缺少依赖：$dependency，请查看 README。" >&2; exit 1; }
done
python3 - <<'PY'
import socket
for port in (18080, 5173):
    with socket.socket() as sock:
        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        try: sock.bind(('127.0.0.1', port))
        except OSError: raise SystemExit(f'端口 {port} 已被占用，请先关闭占用该端口的程序。')
PY
cmake -S "$museflow_root/backend" -B "$museflow_root/build" -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Debug
cmake --build "$museflow_root/build" --parallel "${MUSEFLOW_BUILD_JOBS:-2}"
cd "$museflow_root/frontend"
if [[ ! -d node_modules ]]; then npm ci --no-audit --no-fund; fi
museflow_backend_pid=''
museflow_frontend_pid=''
cleanup() {
  trap - EXIT INT TERM
  if [[ -n "$museflow_frontend_pid" ]]; then kill -- "-$museflow_frontend_pid" 2>/dev/null || true; fi
  if [[ -n "$museflow_backend_pid" ]]; then kill "$museflow_backend_pid" 2>/dev/null || true; fi
  wait 2>/dev/null || true
}
trap cleanup EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM
"$museflow_root/build/museflow" &
museflow_backend_pid=$!
museflow_ready=0
for ((attempt=0; attempt<60; attempt++)); do
  kill -0 "$museflow_backend_pid" 2>/dev/null || { echo '后端启动失败。' >&2; exit 1; }
  if curl --noproxy '*' --fail --silent http://127.0.0.1:18080/api/health >/dev/null; then museflow_ready=1; break; fi
  sleep 0.1
done
[[ "$museflow_ready" == 1 ]] || { echo '等待后端启动超时。' >&2; exit 1; }
setsid npm run dev &
museflow_frontend_pid=$!
echo 'MuseFlow 开发模式：http://127.0.0.1:5173（Ctrl+C 同时关闭前后端）'
wait -n "$museflow_backend_pid" "$museflow_frontend_pid"
scripts/elevenlabs_fixture.py （58 行）
"""Local ElevenLabs contract fixture. Never contacts the real provider."""
import json
import subprocess
import threading
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

class ElevenLabsFixture:
    expected_key = 'fixture-elevenlabs-secret'
    def __init__(self, port=0):
        self.requests = []
        self.scenario = 'success'
        self.mp3 = subprocess.run(['ffmpeg', '-nostdin', '-v', 'error', '-i', str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'assets/mock-demo.wav'), '-f', 'mp3', 'pipe:1'], capture_output=True, check=True).stdout
        owner = self
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def log_message(self, *_): pass
            def respond(self, status, body, mime='application/json'):
                if not isinstance(body, bytes): body = json.dumps(body).encode()
                self.send_response(status)
                self.send_header('Content-Type', mime)
                self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(body)))
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(body)
            def do_POST(self):
                body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers['Content-Length'])))
                owner.requests.append((self.path, body))
                assert not self.headers.get('Authorization') and not self.headers.get('x-goog-api-key')
                assert owner.expected_key not in self.path and owner.expected_key not in json.dumps(body)
                if self.headers.get('xi-api-key') != owner.expected_key:
                    self.respond(401, {'detail': {'status': 'invalid_api_key'}}); return
                scenario = owner.scenario
                if scenario.startswith('http-'):
                    self.respond(int(scenario[5:]), {'detail': {'message': owner.expected_key}}); return
                if scenario == 'paid-plan':
                    self.respond(402, {'detail': {'code': 'paid_plan_required', 'message': owner.expected_key}}); return
                if scenario == 'permissions':
                    self.respond(401, {'detail': {'status': 'missing_permissions', 'message': owner.expected_key}}); return
                if scenario == 'redirect':
                    self.send_response(307); self.send_header('Location', '/must-not-follow'); self.send_header('Content-Length', '0'); self.end_headers(); return
                if self.path == '/v1/music/plan':
                    assert body == {'prompt': 'A gentle solo piano instrumental.', 'music_length_ms': 3000, 'model_id': 'music_v1'}
                    if scenario == 'escaped-key':
                        escaped = ''.join('\\u%04x' % ord(c) for c in owner.expected_key)
                        self.respond(200, ('{"sections":[{"lines":["'+escaped+'"]}]}').encode()); return
                    self.respond(200, {'sections': [{'section_name': 'Intro', 'duration_ms': 3000, 'lines': []}]}); return
                assert self.path == '/v1/music?output_format=mp3_44100_128'
                assert set(body) == {'prompt', 'music_length_ms', 'model_id', 'force_instrumental'}
                assert body['model_id'] == 'music_v1' and isinstance(body['force_instrumental'], bool)
                assert 3000 <= body['music_length_ms'] <= 600000 and len(body['prompt']) <= 4100
                if scenario == 'wrong-mime': self.respond(200, {}, 'text/html'); return
                if scenario == 'invalid-audio': self.respond(200, b'not an mp3', 'audio/mpeg'); return
                if scenario == 'echo-key': self.respond(200, owner.expected_key.encode(), 'audio/mpeg'); return
                self.respond(200, owner.mp3, 'audio/mpeg')
        self.server = ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', port), Handler)
        self.endpoint = f'http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}'
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    def __enter__(self): self.thread.start(); return self
    def __exit__(self, *_): self.server.shutdown(); self.server.server_close(); self.thread.join()
scripts/elevenlabs_test.py （101 行）
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Exercise the actual C++ ElevenLabs provider and audio pipeline against localhost."""
import copy
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from elevenlabs_fixture import ElevenLabsFixture
from smoke_test import ROOT, call, wait_ready, stop
from provider_test import text_job

def main():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='museflow-elevenlabs-test-') as directory, ElevenLabsFixture() as fixture:
        env = {k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if k not in ('DEEPSEEK_API_KEY', 'GEMINI_API_KEY', 'SONGAPI_KEY', 'ELEVENLABS_API_KEY') and k.lower() not in ('http_proxy','https_proxy','all_proxy','no_proxy')}
        env.update(XDG_DATA_HOME=directory+'/data', XDG_CONFIG_HOME=directory+'/config', XDG_CACHE_HOME=directory+'/cache', NO_PROXY='127.0.0.1', MUSEFLOW_ELEVENLABS_TEST_BASE=fixture.endpoint)
        process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT/'build/museflow')], env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        try:
            wait_ready(process)
            def settings(**patch): return call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'music': patch})['music']
            settings(provider='lyria', apiKey='fixture-google-only')
            settings(provider='songapi', apiKey='fixture-songapi-only')
            assert not settings(provider='elevenlabs')['hasApiKey']
            prefs = settings(apiKey=fixture.expected_key, outputFormat='wav')
            assert prefs['keySource'] == 'session' and prefs['activeModel'] == 'music_v1'
            assert text_job('/api/providers/music/test', {})['ok']
            assert len(fixture.requests) == 1 and fixture.requests[0][0] == '/v1/music/plan'
            assert call('/api/settings')['music']['status'] == 'connected'
            fixture.scenario = 'escaped-key'
            text_job('/api/providers/music/test', {}, 'unsafe_response')
            fixture.scenario = 'success'
            blueprint = call('/api/blueprints/generate', 'POST', {'userInput':'温柔钢琴音乐', 'purpose':'game'})['blueprint']
            project = call('/api/projects', 'POST', {'name':'ElevenLabs test', 'blueprint':blueprint}, expected=201)
            def generate(mode='full', strict=False, vocal=False, target=5.123, failure=None, long_prompt=False):
                b = copy.deepcopy(blueprint)
                b.update(targetDurationSeconds=target, strictDuration=strict, vocalMode='vocal' if vocal else 'instrumental', lyrics='今晚星光温柔' if vocal else '')
                if long_prompt: b['sceneDescription'] = 'a' * 4200
                b['structure'] = [dict(b['structure'][0], startSeconds=0, endSeconds=target)]
                started = time.monotonic()
                task = call('/api/generations', 'POST', {'projectId':project['id'], 'blueprint':b, 'mode':mode}, expected=202)
                assert time.monotonic() - started < 1
                for _ in range(400):
                    task = call('/api/generations/'+task['id'])
                    call('/api/health')
                    if task['status'] in ('completed','failed'): break
                    time.sleep(.03)
                assert fixture.expected_key not in json.dumps(task)
                if failure:
                    assert task['status'] == 'failed' and failure in task['error'], task
                    call('/api/generations/'+task['id']+'/audio', expected=409)
                else:
                    assert task['status'] == 'completed', task
                    assert task['provider'] == 'elevenlabs' and task['model'] == 'music_v1'
                return task
            first = generate(strict=True)
            assert abs(first['actualDurationSeconds'] - 5.123) <= 1/44100 and first['format'] == 'wav' and call('/api/versions')[-1]['sourceFormat'] == 'mp3'
            assert fixture.requests[-1][1]['music_length_ms'] == 5123 and fixture.requests[-1][1]['force_instrumental'] is True
            settings(outputFormat='mp3')
            preview = generate(mode='preview', vocal=True)
            assert preview['format'] == 'mp3' and preview['modelTargetDurationSeconds'] == 30
            sent = fixture.requests[-1][1]
            assert sent['music_length_ms'] == 30000 and sent['force_instrumental'] is False and '今晚星光温柔' in sent['prompt'] and '[0s–30s]' in sent['prompt']
            audio = call('/api/generations/'+preview['id']+'/audio', raw=True)
            assert call('/api/generations/'+preview['id']+'/audio', headers={'Range':'bytes=0-9'}, expected=206, raw=True) == audio[:10]
            count = len(call('/api/versions'))
            for scenario, message in [('paid-plan','付费套餐'), ('permissions','Music 权限'), ('http-401','API Key'), ('http-402','额度'), ('http-403','权限'), ('http-429','额度'), ('http-503','不可用'), ('redirect','重定向'), ('wrong-mime','响应类型'), ('invalid-audio','MP3'), ('echo-key','敏感凭据')]:
                fixture.scenario = scenario
                before = len(fixture.requests)
                generate(failure=message)
                assert len(fixture.requests) == before + 1, 'Never retry a paid request or follow redirects'
                assert len(call('/api/versions')) == count
            fixture.scenario = 'success'
            before = len(fixture.requests); generate(long_prompt=True, failure='4100')
            assert len(fixture.requests) == before
            settings(clearApiKey=True)
            call('/api/generations', 'POST', {'projectId':project['id'], 'blueprint':blueprint, 'mode':'full'}, expected=400)
            assert settings(provider='songapi')['hasApiKey'] and settings(provider='lyria')['hasApiKey']
            assert not settings(provider='elevenlabs')['hasApiKey']
            settings(apiKey=fixture.expected_key)
            stop(process)
            assert fixture.expected_key.encode() not in process.stdout.read(); process.stdout.close()
            for file in Path(directory).rglob('*'):
                if file.is_file(): assert fixture.expected_key.encode() not in file.read_bytes(), file
            process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT/'build/museflow')], env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            wait_ready(process)
            assert not call('/api/settings')['music']['hasApiKey']
            assert call('/api/generations/'+preview['id']+'/audio', raw=True) == audio
            stop(process); process.stdout.close()
            env['ELEVENLABS_API_KEY'] = fixture.expected_key
            process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT/'build/museflow')], env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            wait_ready(process)
            assert call('/api/settings')['music']['keySource'] == 'environment'
            assert not settings(clearApiKey=True)['hasApiKey']
            assert settings(useEnvironmentKey=True)['keySource'] == 'environment'
            assert text_job('/api/providers/music/test', {})['ok']
            print('PASS: ElevenLabs auth/plan/generation, preview/vocals/ms duration, MP3/WAV/Range, errors/no retries, prompt limits, independent keys, no key persistence, restart and environment')
        finally:
            stop(process)
            if process.stdout: process.stdout.close()
if __name__ == '__main__': main()
scripts/generate_mock_audio.py （33 行）
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Rebuild MuseFlow's original, deterministic 8-second PCM demonstration asset."""
from pathlib import Path
import math
import struct
import wave

destination = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'assets' / 'mock-demo.wav'
sample_rate = 22050
chords = [(146.832, 174.614, 220.000), (130.813, 164.814, 195.998),
          (116.541, 146.832, 174.614), (130.813, 164.814, 220.000)]
frames = bytearray()
for index in range(sample_rate * 8):
    t = index / sample_rate
    chord_index = min(3, int(t / 2))
    local = t % 2
    chord = chords[chord_index]
    envelope = min(1, local / .1) * min(1, (2 - local) / .25)
    pad = sum(math.sin(2 * math.pi * frequency * t) for frequency in chord) / 3
    note_t = t % .5
    frequency = chord[int(local / .5) % 3] * 2
    melody = math.sin(2 * math.pi * frequency * note_t) * math.exp(-note_t * 6) * min(1, note_t / .008)
    fade = min(1, t / .2) * min(1, (8 - t) / .8)
    value = (.23 * pad * envelope + .20 * melody) * fade
    left = int(value * 32767)
    right = int(value * (.96 + .04 * math.sin(t * .7)) * 32767)
    frames.extend(struct.pack('<hh', left, right))
with wave.open(str(destination), 'wb') as audio:
    audio.setnchannels(2)
    audio.setsampwidth(2)
    audio.setframerate(sample_rate)
    audio.writeframes(frames)
print(f'Created {destination.name}: 8.0 s, stereo PCM WAV')
