scripts/install_desktop.py （35 行）
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Install only this user's launcher and icon; keep all project data unchanged."""
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shlex
import shutil
import subprocess
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if not (ROOT / 'build/museflow-desktop').is_file() or not (ROOT / 'frontend/dist/index.html').is_file():
    raise SystemExit('请先运行 scripts/build.sh')
home = Path.home()
xdg = Path(os.environ.get('XDG_DATA_HOME', str(home / '.local/share')))
if not xdg.is_absolute(): xdg = home / '.local/share'
bin_dir = home / '.local/bin'
applications = xdg / 'applications'
icons = xdg / 'icons/hicolor/scalable/apps'
for directory in (bin_dir, applications, icons): directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
launcher = bin_dir / 'museflow'
marker = '# MuseFlow managed launcher'
if launcher.exists() and marker not in launcher.read_text(): raise SystemExit('已有同名启动程序，已保留；可直接使用项目 scripts/run.sh')
entry = applications / 'museflow.desktop'
if entry.exists() and 'X-MuseFlow-Managed=true' not in entry.read_text(): raise SystemExit('已有同名桌面入口，已保留')
icon = icons / 'museflow.svg'
if icon.exists() and not entry.exists() and icon.read_bytes() != (ROOT / 'desktop/resources/museflow.svg').read_bytes(): raise SystemExit('已有同名图标，已保留')
launcher.write_text('#!/usr/bin/env bash\n' + marker + '\nexec ' + shlex.quote(str(ROOT / 'scripts/run.sh')) + ' "$@"\n')
launcher.chmod(0o755)
# Desktop Entry has both string escaping and Exec argument escaping.
def argument(value):
    escaped = ''.join('\\' + c if c in '\\"`$' else c for c in value)
    return '"' + escaped.replace('\\', '\\\\').replace('%', '%%') + '"'
entry.write_text('[Desktop Entry]\nType=Application\nName=MuseFlow\nName[zh_CN]=MuseFlow 音乐创作\nComment=本地 AI 场景配乐工作台\nExec=' + argument(str(launcher)) + '\nIcon=museflow\nTerminal=false\nCategories=AudioVideo;Audio;\nStartupNotify=true\nStartupWMClass=museflow-desktop\nX-MuseFlow-Managed=true\n')
shutil.copyfile(ROOT / 'desktop/resources/museflow.svg', icons / 'museflow.svg')
if shutil.which('update-desktop-database'): subprocess.run(['update-desktop-database', str(applications)], check=False)
print('已安装应用菜单入口：' + str(entry))
print('启动命令：' + str(launcher))
scripts/music_fixture.py （71 行）
"""Official Interactions REST shape, served locally with synthetic audio and dummy credentials."""
import base64
import copy
import json
import threading
import time
import subprocess
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

class MusicFixture:
    expected_key = 'fixture-session-google'
    def __init__(self, port=0):
        self.scenario = 'success'; self.requests = []; self.polls = 0
        self.wav = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'assets/mock-demo.wav').read_bytes()
        self.mp3 = subprocess.run(['ffmpeg', '-nostdin', '-v', 'error', '-f', 'wav', '-i', 'pipe:0', '-f', 'mp3', 'pipe:1'], input=self.wav, capture_output=True, check=True).stdout
        owner = self
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def log_message(self, *_): pass
            def respond(self, status, payload):
                content = json.dumps(payload).encode()
                self.send_response(status); self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/json'); self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(content))); self.end_headers()
                try: self.wfile.write(content)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError): pass
            def authorized(self):
                assert owner.expected_key not in self.path and not self.headers.get('Authorization')
                if self.headers.get('x-goog-api-key') != owner.expected_key:
                    self.respond(401, {'error': {'message': 'invalid dummy key'}}); return False
                return True
            def do_GET(self):
                if not self.authorized(): return
                if self.path.startswith('/v1beta/models/'):
                    self.respond(200, {'name': self.path.removeprefix('/v1beta/')}); return
                if self.path.startswith('/v1beta/interactions/'):
                    owner.polls += 1; self.respond(200, owner.output()); return
                self.respond(404, {})
            def do_POST(self):
                if not self.authorized(): return
                body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers['Content-Length'])))
                owner.requests.append(copy.deepcopy(body))
                assert self.path == '/v1beta/interactions', self.path
                assert set(body) <= {'model', 'input', 'response_format'}
                assert body['model'] in ('lyria-3-clip-preview', 'lyria-3.5') and isinstance(body['input'], str)
                if 'response_format' in body:
                    assert body['model'] == 'lyria-3.5' and body['response_format'] == {'type': 'audio'}
                time.sleep(.2)
                scenario = owner.scenario
                if scenario.startswith('http-'):
                    self.respond(int(scenario[5:]), {'error': {'message': owner.expected_key}}); return
                if scenario == 'async':
                    self.respond(200, {'id': 'v1_fixture-task', 'status': 'in_progress', 'steps': []}); return
                if scenario == 'unsafe-id':
                    self.respond(200, {'id': '../../secret', 'status': 'queued'}); return
                self.respond(200, owner.output())
        self.server = ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', port), Handler)
        self.endpoint = f'http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/v1beta'
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    def output(self):
        content = [{'type': 'text', 'text': '温暖的回家旋律'}, {'type': 'text', 'text': json.dumps({'sections': [{'name': 'intro', 'start': 0, 'end': 8}]})}, {'type': 'audio', 'mime_type': 'audio/wav', 'data': base64.b64encode(self.wav).decode()}]
        scenario = self.scenario
        if scenario == 'mp3-source': content[-1].update(mime_type='audio/mpeg', data=base64.b64encode(self.mp3).decode())
        if scenario == 'invalid-base64': content[-1]['data'] = '***='
        if scenario == 'invalid-padding': content[-1]['data'] = 'AB=='
        if scenario == 'invalid-wav': content[-1]['data'] = base64.b64encode(b'not-a-wav-file').decode()
        if scenario == 'unsupported-mime': content[-1]['mime_type'] = 'text/html'
        if scenario == 'no-audio': content = content[:-1]
        if scenario == 'echo-key': content[0]['text'] = self.expected_key
        if scenario == 'escaped-key': content[0]['text'] = json.dumps({'key': ''.join('\\u%04x' % ord(c) for c in self.expected_key)}).replace('\\\\u', '\\u')
        return {'id': 'v1_fixture-task', 'status': 'failed' if scenario == 'refused' else 'completed', 'steps': [{'type': 'user_input', 'content': []}, {'type': 'model_output', 'content': content}]}
    def __enter__(self): self.thread.start(); return self
    def __exit__(self, *_): self.server.shutdown(); self.server.server_close(); self.thread.join()
scripts/music_test.py （98 行）
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""C++ Lyria + FFmpeg integration: local fixture only, never a paid API request."""
import io
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import wave
from music_fixture import MusicFixture
from smoke_test import ROOT, call, wait_ready, stop
from provider_test import text_job

def duration_and_samples(data):
    with wave.open(io.BytesIO(data)) as audio:
        return audio.getnframes() / audio.getframerate(), audio.readframes(audio.getnframes())

def main():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='museflow-music-test-') as directory, MusicFixture() as fixture:
        env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k not in ('DEEPSEEK_API_KEY', 'GEMINI_API_KEY') and k.lower() not in ('http_proxy', 'https_proxy', 'all_proxy', 'no_proxy')}
        env.update(XDG_DATA_HOME=directory + '/data', XDG_CONFIG_HOME=directory + '/config', XDG_CACHE_HOME=directory + '/cache', NO_PROXY='127.0.0.1', MUSEFLOW_GOOGLE_TEST_BASE=fixture.endpoint)
        process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'build/museflow')], env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        try:
            wait_ready(process)
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'music': {'provider': 'lyria', 'apiKey': fixture.expected_key, 'outputFormat': 'wav'}})
            assert text_job('/api/providers/music/test', {})['ok']
            assert not fixture.requests, 'Key test must not generate music'
            assert call('/api/settings')['music']['status'] == 'connected'
            blueprint = call('/api/blueprints/generate', 'POST', {'userInput': '音乐测试', 'purpose': 'game'})['blueprint']
            project = call('/api/projects', 'POST', {'name': 'Lyria 契约测试', 'blueprint': blueprint}, expected=201)
            def generate(mode='full', target=12, strict=False, failure=None):
                b = json.loads(json.dumps(blueprint)); b['targetDurationSeconds'] = target; b['strictDuration'] = strict
                b['structure'] = [dict(b['structure'][0], startSeconds=0, endSeconds=target)]
                started = time.monotonic()
                task = call('/api/generations', 'POST', {'projectId': project['id'], 'blueprint': b, 'mode': mode}, expected=202)
                assert time.monotonic() - started < 1
                for _ in range(400):
                    task = call('/api/generations/' + task['id'])
                    started = time.monotonic(); call('/api/health'); assert time.monotonic() - started < 1
                    if task['status'] in ('completed', 'failed'): break
                    time.sleep(.04)
                assert fixture.expected_key not in json.dumps(task)
                if failure:
                    assert task['status'] == 'failed' and failure in task['error'], task
                    call('/api/generations/' + task['id'] + '/audio', expected=409)
                else: assert task['status'] == 'completed', task
                return task
            first = generate()
            assert first['sourceDurationSeconds'] == 8 and first['actualDurationSeconds'] == 8 and first['provider'] == 'lyria'
            assert first['songText'][0] == '温暖的回家旋律' and first['songStructure'][0]['sections'][0]['name'] == 'intro'
            assert fixture.requests[-1]['model'] == 'lyria-3.5' and fixture.requests[-1]['response_format'] == {'type': 'audio'}
            for target in (5, 12, 5.123):
                task = generate(target=target, strict=True)
                duration, samples = duration_and_samples(call('/api/generations/' + task['id'] + '/audio', raw=True))
                assert abs(duration - target) <= 1 / 44100
                assert task['strictDurationApplied'] and task['sourceDurationSeconds'] == 8
                if target == 12: assert samples[-44100:] == bytes(44100), 'Padded ending must be silent'
                if target == 5:
                    import array
                    values = array.array('h', samples[-400:]); assert max(map(abs, values)) < 100, 'Trimmed ending must fade'
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'music': {'outputFormat': 'mp3'}})
            mp3 = generate('preview')
            assert mp3['format'] == 'mp3' and mp3['modelTargetDurationSeconds'] == 30
            assert fixture.requests[-1]['model'] == 'lyria-3-clip-preview' and 'response_format' not in fixture.requests[-1]
            assert '30' in fixture.requests[-1]['input']
            data = call('/api/generations/' + mp3['id'] + '/audio', raw=True)
            assert data[:3] == b'ID3' or data[:1] == b'\xff'
            assert call('/api/generations/' + mp3['id'] + '/audio', headers={'Range': 'bytes=0-9'}, expected=206, raw=True) == data[:10]
            exact = generate('preview', strict=True)
            assert exact['format'] == 'wav' and exact['actualDurationSeconds'] == 30, 'Strict MP3 preference must select exact WAV'
            fixture.scenario = 'mp3-source'; source_mp3 = generate(target=5, strict=True)
            assert source_mp3['actualDurationSeconds'] == 5 and source_mp3['sourceDurationSeconds'] > 8
            fixture.scenario = 'async'; generate(); assert fixture.polls
            count = len(call('/api/versions'))
            for scenario, message in [('invalid-base64', 'Base64'), ('invalid-padding', 'Base64'), ('invalid-wav', 'FFmpeg'), ('unsupported-mime', 'MIME'), ('no-audio', '未返回音频'), ('refused', '未完成'), ('unsafe-id', '任务 ID'), ('echo-key', '敏感凭据'), ('escaped-key', '敏感凭据'), ('http-401', 'API Key'), ('http-403', '权限'), ('http-429', '额度'), ('http-503', '不可用')]:
                fixture.scenario = scenario; before = len(fixture.requests); generate(failure=message)
                assert len(fixture.requests) == before + 1, 'Paid generation must never automatically retry'
                assert len(call('/api/versions')) == count, 'Failed generation must not save a version'
            fixture.scenario = 'success'
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'music': {'clearApiKey': True}})
            call('/api/generations', 'POST', {'projectId': project['id'], 'blueprint': blueprint, 'mode': 'full'}, expected=400)
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'music': {'provider': 'mock', 'outputFormat': 'wav'}})
            mock = generate(target=5, strict=True)
            assert mock['provider'] == 'mock' and mock['actualDurationSeconds'] == 5
            stop(process); assert fixture.expected_key.encode() not in process.stdout.read(); process.stdout.close()
            for file in Path(directory).rglob('*'):
                if file.is_file(): assert fixture.expected_key.encode() not in file.read_bytes(), file
            process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'build/museflow')], env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            wait_ready(process)
            assert not call('/api/settings')['music']['hasApiKey']
            assert call('/api/generations/' + first['id'])['songStructure']
            assert call('/api/generations/' + mp3['id'] + '/audio', raw=True) == data
            print('PASS: Lyria Interactions POST/GET and key metadata test, MP3/WAV, lyrics/structure/Base64, strict trim/pad/fade/sample precision, preview timeline, async health, failure/no-paid-retry, key secrecy, restart')
        finally:
            stop(process)
            if process.stdout: process.stdout.close()
if __name__ == '__main__': main()
scripts/provider_fixture.py （75 行）
"""Local Chat Completions contract fixture. No real model or external network access."""
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
import json
import threading
import time

def blueprint():
    return {
        'id': 'model-draft', 'title': '契约测试配乐', 'sceneDescription': '旅人走进雨后的森林', 'purpose': 'game',
        'genre': '电影配乐', 'subGenre': 'Ambient', 'mood': ['希望'], 'bpm': 96, 'key': 'D minor',
        'instruments': ['钢琴', '弦乐'], 'vocalMode': 'instrumental', 'vocalGender': '', 'language': '中文', 'lyrics': '',
        'targetDurationSeconds': 60, 'strictDuration': False,
        'structure': [{'id': 'opening', 'name': '序章', 'startSeconds': 0, 'endSeconds': 30, 'emotion': '静谧', 'intensity': 3, 'instruments': ['钢琴'], 'description': '钢琴渐入'},
                      {'id': 'ending', 'name': '尾声', 'startSeconds': 30, 'endSeconds': 60, 'emotion': '希望', 'intensity': 6, 'instruments': ['弦乐'], 'description': '弦乐展开并收尾'}],
        'negativePrompt': '刺耳高频', 'finalLyriaPrompt': 'A hopeful cinematic forest cue with piano and strings, 96 BPM in D minor. Target 60 seconds: sparse piano at 0–30s, hopeful strings at 30–60s. Instrumental; avoid harsh high frequencies.'
    }

class TextFixture:
    def __init__(self, port=0):
        fixture = self
        self.counts = {}
        self.paths = []
        self.expected_key = 'fixture-session-text'
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def log_message(self, *_): pass
            def respond(self, status, data, headers=None):
                content = data if isinstance(data, bytes) else json.dumps(data).encode()
                try:
                    self.send_response(status)
                    self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/json')
                    self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(content)))
                    for name, value in (headers or {}).items(): self.send_header(name, value)
                    self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(content)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError): pass
            def do_POST(self):
                payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers['Content-Length'])))
                model = payload['model']
                with fixture.lock:
                    fixture.counts[model] = fixture.counts.get(model, 0) + 1
                    count = fixture.counts[model]
                    fixture.paths.append(self.path)
                if self.headers.get('Authorization') != 'Bearer ' + fixture.expected_key:
                    self.respond(401, {'error': 'bad fixture key'}); return
                if payload.get('response_format') != {'type': 'json_object'} or payload.get('stream') is not False:
                    self.respond(400, {'error': 'request contract mismatch'}); return
                if model.startswith('http-'):
                    self.respond(int(model[5:]), {'error': fixture.expected_key}); return
                if model == 'redirect':
                    self.respond(302, {}, {'Location': fixture.endpoint + '/redirect-target'}); return
                if model == 'invalid-envelope': self.respond(200, b'not-json'); return
                if model == 'missing-choices': self.respond(200, {}); return
                if model == 'too-deep': self.respond(200, b'[' * 70 + b'0' + b']' * 70); return
                if model == 'oversized': self.respond(200, b'x' * (2 * 1024 * 1024 + 10)); return
                if model in ('slow', 'timeout'): time.sleep(3)
                messages = payload['messages']
                test = 'Connection test.' in messages[-1]['content']
                data = {'ok': True} if test else blueprint()
                if not test and '请修改当前设计稿' in messages[1]['content']:
                    data['bpm'] = 112; data['vocalMode'] = 'vocal'; data['vocalGender'] = '轻柔女声'; data['lyrics'] = '雨后的光，带我回家'
                    data['finalLyriaPrompt'] = 'Revised cinematic song with soft female vocals in Chinese at 112 BPM. Lyrics: 雨后的光，带我回家'
                if model in ('invalid-schema', 'retry-once') and (model == 'invalid-schema' or count == 1): data['bpm'] = 'fast'
                if model == 'overlap': data['structure'][1]['startSeconds'] = 20
                if model == 'unknown-property': data['notAllowed'] = 'value'
                if model in ('echo-key', 'echo-escaped-key'): data['sceneDescription'] = fixture.expected_key
                content = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)
                if model == 'echo-escaped-key': content = content.replace(fixture.expected_key, '\\u0066' + fixture.expected_key[1:])
                if model == 'blank-once' and count == 1: content = ''
                reason = 'content_filter' if model == 'refusal' else 'length' if model == 'truncated' else 'stop'
                self.respond(200, {'choices': [{'finish_reason': reason, 'message': {'role': 'assistant', 'content': content}}]})
        self.server = ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', port), Handler)
        self.endpoint = f'http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}'
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    def __enter__(self): self.thread.start(); return self
    def __exit__(self, *_): self.server.shutdown(); self.server.server_close(); self.thread.join()
scripts/provider_test.py （103 行）
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Exercises the real C++ libcurl provider against a local contract server, with dummy keys only."""
from pathlib import Path
import json
import os
import socket
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from provider_fixture import TextFixture
from smoke_test import ROOT, call, wait_ready, stop

def text_job(path, payload, expected_code=None):
    started = time.monotonic()
    accepted = call(path, 'POST', payload, expected=202)
    assert time.monotonic() - started < 1, 'HTTP handler waited on upstream model'
    job_id = accepted['job']['id']
    for _ in range(200):
        job = call('/api/text-jobs/' + job_id)
        started = time.monotonic(); call('/api/health')
        assert time.monotonic() - started < 1, 'Health blocked by model request'
        if job['status'] in ('completed', 'failed'): break
        time.sleep(.04)
    assert 'fixture-session-text' not in json.dumps(job), 'Key leaked in public job'
    if expected_code:
        assert job['status'] == 'failed' and job['code'] == expected_code, job
        return job
    assert job['status'] == 'completed', job
    return job['result']

def main():
    with socket.socket() as probe:
        probe.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        probe.bind(('127.0.0.1', 18080))
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='museflow-provider-test-') as directory, TextFixture() as fixture:
        env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k not in ('DEEPSEEK_API_KEY', 'GEMINI_API_KEY') and k.lower() not in ('http_proxy', 'https_proxy', 'all_proxy', 'no_proxy')}
        env.update(XDG_DATA_HOME=directory + '/data', XDG_CONFIG_HOME=directory + '/config', XDG_CACHE_HOME=directory + '/cache', NO_PROXY='127.0.0.1')
        process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'build/museflow')], env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        try:
            wait_ready(process)
            assert call('/api/health')['phase'] == 4
            initial = call('/api/settings')
            assert initial['text']['hasApiKey'] is False
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'baseUrl': 'https://invalid.example?key=secret'}}, expected=400)
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'baseUrl': 'file:///etc/passwd'}}, expected=400)
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'apiKey': 'header\ninjection'}}, expected=400)
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'apiKey': 'secret'}}, headers={'Origin': 'https://evil.example'}, expected=403)
            prefs = call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'provider': 'deepseek', 'baseUrl': fixture.endpoint + '/v1', 'model': 'custom-model', 'apiKey': fixture.expected_key}, 'music': {'apiKey': 'fixture-music-key', 'model': 'lyria-3.5', 'outputFormat': 'mp3'}})
            assert prefs['text']['keySource'] == 'session' and 'apiKey' not in prefs['text']
            assert prefs['music']['provider'] == 'mock' and prefs['music']['activeOutputFormat'] == 'mp3'
            assert 'fixture-' not in json.dumps(prefs)
            assert text_job('/api/providers/text/test', {})['ok'] is True
            assert call('/api/settings')['text']['status'] == 'connected'
            generated = text_job('/api/blueprints/generate', {'userInput': '希望的森林', 'purpose': 'game'})['blueprint']
            assert generated['promptSource'] == 'deepseek' and generated['bpm'] == 96
            assert fixture.paths[-1] == '/v1/chat/completions'
            saved = call('/api/projects', 'POST', {'name': 'AI 草稿', 'blueprint': generated}, expected=201)
            assert saved['blueprint']['finalLyriaPrompt'] == generated['finalLyriaPrompt'], 'Saved AI prompt overwritten'
            revised = text_job('/api/blueprints/revise', {'blueprint': generated, 'instruction': '加入中文人声，BPM 改为112'})['blueprint']
            assert revised['vocalMode'] == 'vocal' and revised['lyrics'] and revised['bpm'] == 112
            assert revised['id'] != generated['id']
            generated['bpm'] = 110
            validated = call('/api/blueprints/validate', 'POST', {'blueprint': generated})['blueprint']
            assert validated['promptSource'] == 'local' and '110 BPM' in validated['finalLyriaPrompt']
            cases = [('retry-once', None, 2), ('blank-once', None, 2), ('invalid-schema', 'invalid_blueprint', 2), ('overlap', 'invalid_blueprint', 2), ('unknown-property', 'invalid_blueprint', 2), ('http-401', 'unauthorized', 1), ('http-402', 'insufficient_balance', 1), ('http-403', 'forbidden', 1), ('http-429', 'rate_limited', 1), ('http-503', 'upstream_error', 1), ('redirect', 'redirect', 1), ('refusal', 'refused', 1), ('truncated', 'truncated', 1), ('invalid-envelope', 'invalid_response', 1), ('missing-choices', 'invalid_response', 1), ('oversized', 'response_too_large', 1), ('echo-key', 'unsafe_response', 1)]
            for model, code, count in cases:
                call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'model': model}})
                text_job('/api/blueprints/generate', {'userInput': '测试场景', 'purpose': 'game'}, code)
                assert fixture.counts[model] == count, (model, fixture.counts)
            for model, code in [('http-422', 'bad_request'), ('echo-escaped-key', 'unsafe_response'), ('too-deep', 'invalid_response')]:
                call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'model': model}})
                text_job('/api/blueprints/generate', {'userInput': '测试场景', 'purpose': 'game'}, code)
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'model': 'timeout', 'connectTimeoutSeconds': 1, 'requestTimeoutSeconds': 2}})
            text_job('/api/blueprints/generate', {'userInput': '测试场景', 'purpose': 'game'}, 'timeout')
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'clearApiKey': True}})
            assert call('/api/settings')['text']['hasApiKey'] is False
            text_job('/api/providers/text/test', {}, 'missing_key')
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'model': 'custom-model', 'apiKey': fixture.expected_key}})
            stop(process)
            assert b'fixture-' not in process.stdout.read()
            process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'build/museflow')], env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            wait_ready(process)
            after = call('/api/settings')
            assert after['text']['model'] == 'custom-model' and not after['text']['hasApiKey'] and not after['music']['hasApiKey']
            assert call('/api/projects')[0]['blueprint']['promptSource'] == 'deepseek'
            for path in Path(directory).rglob('*'):
                if path.is_file(): assert b'fixture-' not in path.read_bytes(), f'Key leaked to {path.name}'
            stop(process)
            env.update(DEEPSEEK_API_KEY=fixture.expected_key, GEMINI_API_KEY='fixture-environment-music', HTTP_PROXY=fixture.endpoint, NO_PROXY='')
            process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'build/museflow')], env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            wait_ready(process)
            assert call('/api/settings')['text']['keySource'] == 'environment'
            assert call('/api/settings')['music']['keySource'] == 'environment'
            # Unreachable target succeeds only if uppercase HTTP_PROXY is honored by the C++ transport.
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'baseUrl': 'http://127.0.0.1:1/v1', 'model': 'proxy-test'}})
            assert text_job('/api/providers/text/test', {})['ok']
            assert fixture.paths[-1] == 'http://127.0.0.1:1/v1/chat/completions'
            print('PASS: real libcurl provider contract, async responsiveness, create/revise/lyrics, schema + bounded retries, 401/402/403/429/503/timeout/refusal/redirect/oversize errors, key redaction and restart, environment keys, HTTP_PROXY')
        finally:
            stop(process)
            if process.stdout: process.stdout.close()

if __name__ == '__main__': main()

scripts/smoke_test.py （108 行）
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""HTTP integration tests; runs a real backend against isolated XDG directories."""
from pathlib import Path
from urllib import request, error
import io
import json
import os
import socket
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import wave

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:18080'
OPENER = request.build_opener(request.ProxyHandler({}))

def call(path, method='GET', payload=None, headers=None, expected=200, raw=False):
    data = None if payload is None else json.dumps(payload).encode()
    req = request.Request(BASE + path, data=data, method=method, headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json', **(headers or {})})
    try: response = OPENER.open(req, timeout=10)
    except error.HTTPError as caught: response = caught
    with response:
        content = response.read()
        assert response.status == expected, (path, response.status, content[:300])
        return content if raw else json.loads(content)

def wait_ready(process):
    for _ in range(100):
        if process.poll() is not None: raise RuntimeError('后端提前退出')
        try:
            if call('/api/health')['status'] == 'ok': return
        except (OSError, error.URLError): pass
        time.sleep(.1)
    raise RuntimeError('后端启动超时')

def stop(process):
    if process.poll() is None:
        process.terminate()
        try: process.wait(5)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            process.kill(); process.wait()

def main():
    with socket.socket() as probe:
        probe.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        try: probe.bind(('127.0.0.1', 18080))
        except OSError: raise SystemExit('集成测试需要独占 18080 端口，请先停止开发服务器。')
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='museflow-http-test-') as directory:
        env = dict(os.environ, XDG_DATA_HOME=directory + '/data', XDG_CONFIG_HOME=directory + '/config', XDG_CACHE_HOME=directory + '/cache')
        process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'build/museflow')], env=env)
        try:
            wait_ready(process)
            assert call('/api/health')['mode'] == 'mock'
            assert call('/api/settings')['capabilities']['strictDuration'] is True
            call('/api/health', headers={'Origin': 'https://evil.example'}, expected=403)
            call('/api/health', headers={'Host': 'evil.example:18080'}, expected=403)
            call('/api/projects/not-a-uuid', expected=400)
            call('/api/unknown', expected=404)
            blueprint = call('/api/blueprints/generate', 'POST', {'userInput': '雨后的森林，温暖的希望', 'purpose': 'game'})['blueprint']
            broken = json.loads(json.dumps(blueprint)); broken['structure'][1]['startSeconds'] = 10
            call('/api/blueprints/validate', 'POST', {'blueprint': broken}, expected=400)
            project = call('/api/projects', 'POST', {'name': '集成测试项目', 'blueprint': blueprint}, expected=201)
            pid = project['id']
            task = call('/api/generations', 'POST', {'projectId': pid, 'blueprint': blueprint, 'mode': 'preview'}, expected=202)
            tid = task['id']
            call(f'/api/generations/{tid}/audio', expected=409)
            call(f'/api/projects/{pid}', 'DELETE', expected=400)
            duplicate = subprocess.run([str(ROOT / 'build/museflow')], env=env, capture_output=True, timeout=5)
            assert duplicate.returncode != 0, 'Duplicate instance must not open the live database'
            assert call(f'/api/generations/{tid}')['status'] != 'failed', 'Duplicate startup corrupted an active task'
            states = {'queued'}
            for _ in range(100):
                task = call(f'/api/generations/{tid}')
                states.add(task['status'])
                start = time.monotonic(); call('/api/health')
                assert time.monotonic() - start < 1, 'HTTP was blocked by generation'
                if task['status'] in ('completed', 'failed'): break
                time.sleep(.08)
            assert task['status'] == 'completed', task
            assert {'preparing', 'generating', 'post_processing', 'completed'} <= states, states
            content = call(f'/api/generations/{tid}/audio', raw=True)
            with wave.open(io.BytesIO(content)) as audio:
                assert audio.getnframes() / audio.getframerate() == 8
            assert call(f'/api/generations/{tid}/audio', headers={'Range': 'bytes=0-43'}, expected=206, raw=True) == content[:44]
            assert call(f'/api/generations/{tid}/audio?download=1', raw=True) == content
            version = call(f'/api/projects/{pid}/versions')[0]
            assert version['blueprint']['bpm'] == 88
            blueprint['bpm'] = 112
            call(f'/api/projects/{pid}', 'PUT', {'name': '修改后的项目', 'blueprint': blueprint})
            assert call(f'/api/projects/{pid}/versions')[0]['blueprint']['bpm'] == 88
            stop(process)
            process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'build/museflow')], env=env)
            wait_ready(process)
            assert call(f'/api/projects/{pid}')['blueprint']['bpm'] == 112
            assert call(f'/api/generations/{tid}')['status'] == 'completed'
            assert len(call('/api/versions')) == 1
            assert call(f'/api/generations/{tid}/audio', raw=True) == content
            html = call('/studio', raw=True)
            assert b'<html' in html and b'MuseFlow' in html, 'Production SPA was not served'
            call(f'/api/projects/{pid}', 'DELETE')
            assert not call('/api/projects') and not call('/api/versions')
            call(f'/api/generations/{tid}/audio', expected=404)
            assert not (Path(directory) / 'data/museflow/projects' / pid).exists()
            print('PASS: HTTP workflow, all progress states, nonblocking health, Range/audio/download, validation, local-origin guards, single-instance lock, immutable snapshots, restart, SPA serving, cascade deletion')
        finally: stop(process)

if __name__ == '__main__': main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3

from provider_fixture import TextFixture
from music_fixture import MusicFixture

    raise SystemExit(0)

scripts/versions_test.py （75 行）
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Version lineage, immutable snapshots, deletion, export and restart on an isolated backend."""
import copy
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from smoke_test import ROOT, call, wait_ready, stop

def main():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='museflow-versions-') as directory:
        env = dict(os.environ, XDG_DATA_HOME=directory + '/data', XDG_CONFIG_HOME=directory + '/config', XDG_CACHE_HOME=directory + '/cache')
        for name in ('DEEPSEEK_API_KEY', 'GEMINI_API_KEY', 'MUSEFLOW_GOOGLE_TEST_BASE'): env.pop(name, None)
        process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'build/museflow')], env=env)
        try:
            wait_ready(process)
            b = call('/api/blueprints/generate', 'POST', {'userInput': '分支测试森林', 'purpose': 'game'})['blueprint']
            p = call('/api/projects', 'POST', {'name': '原项目', 'blueprint': b}, expected=201)
            other = call('/api/projects', 'POST', {'name': '另一个项目', 'blueprint': b}, expected=201)
            def generate(blueprint, parent=''):
                return call('/api/generations', 'POST', {'projectId': p['id'], 'blueprint': blueprint, 'mode': 'preview', 'parentVersionId': parent}, expected=202)
            def wait(task):
                for _ in range(160):
                    result = call('/api/generations/' + task['id'])
                    if result['status'] in ('completed', 'failed'): break
                    time.sleep(.05)
                assert result['status'] == 'completed', result
                return call('/api/versions/' + result['versionId'])
            root = wait(generate(b)); root_audio = call('/api/generations/' + root['generationId'] + '/audio', raw=True)
            draft = call('/api/versions/' + root['id'] + '/branch', 'POST', {}, expected=201)['project']
            assert draft['draftParentVersionId'] == root['id'] and draft['blueprint']['id'] != b['id']
            assert len(call('/api/versions')) == 1, 'Branch draft cannot invent a completed audio version'
            b2 = copy.deepcopy(draft['blueprint']); b2['bpm'] = 112
            call('/api/projects/' + p['id'], 'PUT', {'name': '改名项目', 'blueprint': b2, 'draftParentVersionId': root['id']})
            task = generate(b2, root['id'])
            assert call('/api/activity')['musicTasks'] == 1
            call('/api/versions/' + root['id'], 'DELETE', expected=400)
            call('/api/versions/' + root['id'] + '/branch', 'POST', {}, expected=400)
            child = wait(task)
            assert child['parentVersionId'] == root['id'] and child['blueprint']['bpm'] == 112
            assert call('/api/versions/' + root['id'])['blueprint']['bpm'] == 88
            assert call('/api/generations/' + root['generationId'] + '/audio', raw=True) == root_audio
            call('/api/generations', 'POST', {'projectId': other['id'], 'blueprint': b, 'mode': 'full', 'parentVersionId': root['id']}, expected=400)
            call('/api/projects/' + other['id'], 'PUT', {'name': 'invalid parent', 'blueprint': b, 'draftParentVersionId': root['id']}, expected=400)
            call('/api/versions/' + root['id'], 'DELETE', expected=400)
            grandchild = wait(generate(b2, child['id']))
            sibling = wait(generate(b, root['id']))
            assert len(call('/api/projects/' + p['id'] + '/versions')) == 4
            rated = call('/api/versions/' + child['id'] + '/metadata', 'PUT', {'rating': 5, 'notes': '更适合结尾，保留。'})
            assert rated['rating'] == 5 and rated['notes'] and rated['parentVersionId'] == root['id']
            for patch in ({'rating': 6}, {'rating': 1.5}, {'rating': 4294967296}, {'notes': 42}, {'notes': 'a' * 6001}, {'blueprint': b}, {'apiKey': 'fixture-metadata-key'}):
                call('/api/versions/' + child['id'] + '/metadata', 'PUT', patch, expected=400)
            call('/api/settings', 'PUT', {'text': {'apiKey': 'fixture-export-secret'}, 'music': {'apiKey': 'fixture-export-google'}})
            export = call('/api/projects/' + p['id'] + '/export')
            assert export['format'] == 'museflow-project' and not export['includesAudio'] and len(export['versions']) == 4
            assert 'fixture-' not in json.dumps(export) and 'apiKey' not in json.dumps(export)
            stop(process); process = subprocess.Popen([str(ROOT / 'build/museflow')], env=env); wait_ready(process)
            assert call('/api/versions/' + child['id'])['rating'] == 5
            assert call('/api/projects/' + p['id'])['draftParentVersionId'] == root['id']
            assert call('/api/versions/' + grandchild['id'])['parentVersionId'] == child['id']
            for version in (grandchild, sibling, child, root):
                call('/api/versions/' + version['id'], 'DELETE')
                call('/api/versions/' + version['id'], expected=404)
                call('/api/generations/' + version['generationId'] + '/audio', expected=404)
                assert call('/api/generations/' + version['generationId'])['audioDeleted']
                for suffix in ('.wav', '.mp3', '.source.wav', '.source.mp3'):
                    assert not (Path(directory) / 'data/museflow/projects' / p['id'] / (version['generationId'] + suffix)).exists()
            assert call('/api/projects/' + p['id'])['draftParentVersionId'] is None
            assert not call('/api/versions') and call('/api/activity')['musicTasks'] == 0
            assert len(call('/api/projects/' + p['id'])['tasks']) == 4
            print('PASS: root/child/grandchild/sibling lineage, persistent drafts, immutable audio, cross-project guard, active/parent deletion guards, rating validation, export secrecy, restart, leaf deletion and history')
        finally: stop(process)
if __name__ == '__main__': main()
本文档离线可用，无外部请求；代码为提交时的项目快照。构建方法见 README.md。
